POLYGON((-105.223959 21.822541, -105.227021 21.805673, -105.210281 21.805863, -105.187933 21.808973, -105.179200 21.824049, -105.184045 21.829051, -105.207648 21.825306, -105.223959 21.822541))
$zonaCobertura = "POLYGON((-105.223959 21.822541, -105.227021 21.805673, -105.210281 21.805863, -105.187933 21.808973, -105.179200 21.824049, -105.184045 21.829051, -105.207648 21.825306, -105.223959 21.822541))"
# Definir los puntos del polígono [Longitud, Latitud]
$poligono = @(`
    @(-105.223959, 21.822541),`
    @(-105.227021, 21.805673),`
    @(-105.210281, 21.805863),`
    @(-105.187933, 21.808973),`
    @(-105.179200, 21.824049),`
    @(-105.184045, 21.829051),`
    @(-105.207648, 21.825306)`
)
function Test-PointInPolygon {`
    param(`
        [double]$lonCliente,`
        [double]$latCliente,`
        [array]$poly`
    )`
    `
    $inside = $false`
    $j = $poly.Count - 1`
    `
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $poly.Count; $i++) {`
        $xi = $poly[$i][0]; $yi = $poly[$i][1]`
        $xj = $poly[$j][0]; $yj = $poly[$j][1]`
        `
        $intersect = (($yi -gt $latCliente) -ne ($yj -gt $latCliente)) -and`
            ($lonCliente -lt ($xj - xi) * ($latCliente - yi) / ($yj - yi) + $xi)
        if ($intersect) { $inside = -not $inside }
        $j = $i
    }
    return $inside
}
# Prueba con una coordenada de ejemplo [Longitud, Latitud]
$estaAdentro = Test-PointInPolygon -lonCliente -105.204 -latCliente 21.812 -poly $poligono
if ($estaAdentro) {`
    Write-Host "El cliente está dentro de la zona de cobertura." -ForegroundColor Green`
} else {`
    Write-Host "El cliente está fuera de la zona de cobertura." -ForegroundColor Red`
}

    $gzip = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new($file, [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress)`

`
    $line = $reader.ReadLine()`
`

            $obj.PSObject.Properties.Name`
        }`
        catch {`
            Write-Host "El primer registro no es JSON válido."`
        }`
    }`
`
    $reader.Dispose()`
    $gzip.Dispose()`
    $file.Dispose()`
}

# MICROSCOPE: 3 registros por archivo
Get-ChildItem "microscopio_*.jsonl.gz" | Sort-Object Name | ForEach-Object {`
    Write-Host "`n========== $($_.Name) ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $fs = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($_.FullName)`
    $gz = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new($fs, [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress)`
    $sr = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($gz)`
`
    for ($i = 1; $i -le 3; $i++) {`
        $line = $sr.ReadLine()`
        if ($null -eq $line) { break }`
        Write-Host "`nRegistro $i"`
        if ($line.Length -gt 1800) { $line = $line.Substring(0,1800) + " ... [RECORTADO]" }`
        Write-Output $line`
    }`
`
    $sr.Dispose()`
    $gz.Dispose()`
    $fs.Dispose()`
}

function FechaUTC($ts) {`
    if ($ts -gt 0) {`
        return [DateTimeOffset]::FromUnixTimeMilliseconds([long]($ts * 1000)).UtcDateTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")`
    }`
    return "N/D"`
}

$zip = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead((Join-Path (Get-Location) "dataflow_log.zip"))
$entry = $zip.GetEntry("dataflow_log.jsonl")

$sr = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($stream)
$totalD = 0
$errorD = 0
$minD = [double]::MaxValue
$maxD = 0
$campos = [System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]]::new()
while ($null -ne ($line = $sr.ReadLine())) {`
    try {`
        $o = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
        $totalD++`
`
        if ($null -ne $o.ts) {`
            $ts = [double]$o.ts`
            if ($ts -lt $minD) { $minD = $ts }`
            if ($ts -gt $maxD) { $maxD = $ts }`
        }`
`
        foreach ($p in $o.PSObject.Properties.Name) {`
            [void]$campos.Add($p)`
        }`
    }`
    catch {`
        $errorD++`
    }`
}
$sr.Dispose()
$stream.Dispose()
$zip.Dispose()

    (Join-Path (Get-Location) "dataflow_log.zip")`

$filas = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
while ($null -ne ($line = $sr.ReadLine())) {`
    try {`
        $o = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
`
        if ($o.ts -ge $desde -and $o.ts -le $hasta) {`
            $filas.Add([PSCustomObject]@{`
                epoch = $o.epoch`
                elapsed = $o.elapsed`
                tw_sigma = $o.tw_sigma`
                tw_v10 = $o.tw_v10`
                tw_z = $o.tw_z`
                tw_side = $o.tw_side`
                winner = $o.winner`
                regime = $o.regime`
                vote_consensus = $o.vote_consensus`
            })`
        }`
    }`
    catch {}`
}
$sr.Dispose()
$zip.Dispose()
function Get-NumericStats($values) {`
    $nums = @($values | Where-Object {`
        $null -ne $_ -and "$_".Trim() -ne ""`
    } | ForEach-Object { [double]$_ })`
`
    if ($nums.Count -eq 0) {`
        return @{ Min = ""; Avg = ""; Max = "" }`
    }`
`
    $m = $nums | Measure-Object -Minimum -Maximum -Average`
`
    return @{`
        Min = [math]::Round($m.Minimum, 3)`
        Avg = [math]::Round($m.Average, 3)`
        Max = [math]::Round($m.Maximum, 3)`
    }`
}
$reporte = foreach ($grupo in ($filas | Group-Object epoch | Sort-Object Name)) {`
    $g = $grupo.Group`
`
    $sigma = Get-NumericStats $g.tw_sigma`
    $v10 = Get-NumericStats $g.tw_v10`
    $z = Get-NumericStats $g.tw_z`
    $elapsed = Get-NumericStats $g.elapsed`
`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Epoch = $grupo.Name`
        N = $g.Count`
        Elapsed = "$($elapsed.Min)-$($elapsed.Max)"`
        SigmaMin = $sigma.Min`
        SigmaAvg = $sigma.Avg`
        SigmaMax = $sigma.Max`
        V10Min = $v10.Min`
        V10Max = $v10.Max`
        ZMin = $z.Min`
        ZMax = $z.Max`
        TwSide = (@($g.tw_side | Where-Object { $_ } | Sort-Object -Unique) -join ",")`
        Winner = (@($g.winner | Where-Object { $_ } | Sort-Object -Unique) -join ",")`
        Regime = (@($g.regime | Where-Object { $_ } | Sort-Object -Unique) -join ",")`
        Consensus = (@($g.vote_consensus | Where-Object { $_ } | Sort-Object -Unique) -join ",")`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n===== RESUMEN POR EPOCH ====="

Write-Host "Total de epochs: $($reporte.Count)"

$sr = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($f.FullName)
$buffer = New-Object char[] 3000
$n = $sr.Read($buffer, 0, $buffer.Length)
$sr.Dispose()
[System.String]::new($buffer, 0, $n)
$doc = Get-Content ".\result.json" -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$mensajes = foreach ($m in $doc.messages) {`
    if ($m.text -is [array]) {`
        $txt = ($m.text | ForEach-Object {`
            if ($_ -is [string]) { $_ }`
            else { $_.text }`
        }) -join ""`
    }`
    else {`
        $txt = [string]$m.text`
    }`
`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Fecha = $m.date`
        Unix = [double]$m.date_unixtime`
        Texto = $txt`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n===== RESUMEN DEL HISTORIAL ====="
Write-Host "Mensajes totales: $($mensajes.Count)"
Write-Host "Desde: $(($mensajes | Sort-Object Unix | Select-Object -First 1).Fecha)"
Write-Host "Hasta: $(($mensajes | Sort-Object Unix | Select-Object -Last 1).Fecha)"
$ciclos = @($mensajes | Where-Object { $_.Texto -match "Nuevo ciclo" })
$correcciones = @($mensajes | Where-Object { $_.Texto -match "PTB watcher aplicado" })
Write-Host "`n===== TIPOS PRINCIPALES ====="
Write-Host "Inicios de ciclo: $($ciclos.Count)"
Write-Host "Correcciones PTB: $($correcciones.Count)"
Write-Host "`n===== ULTIMOS 5 INICIOS DE CICLO ====="
$ciclos | Sort-Object Unix -Descending | Select-Object -First 5 Fecha, Texto | Format-List
Write-Host "`n===== ULTIMAS 10 CORRECCIONES ====="
$correcciones | Sort-Object Unix -Descending | Select-Object -First 10 Fecha, Texto | Format-List
Write-Host "`n===== MENSAJES QUE NO SON INICIO NI CORRECCION ====="
$mensajes |`
    Where-Object { $_.Texto -notmatch "Nuevo ciclo|PTB watcher aplicado" } |`
    Select-Object -First 10 Fecha, Texto |`
    Format-List
Write-Host "`n===== ACTIVIDAD POR HORA LOCAL ====="
$mensajes |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $fecha = [datetime]::Parse($_.Fecha)`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Hora = $fecha.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:00")`
            Tipo = if ($_.Texto -match "Nuevo ciclo") {`
                "Ciclo"`
            }`
            elseif ($_.Texto -match "PTB watcher aplicado") {`
                "Correccion"`
            }`
            elseif ($_.Texto -match "TF BUY") {`
                "Compra"`
            }`
            else {`
                "Otro"`
            }`
        }`
    } |`
    Group-Object Hora, Tipo |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Grupo = $_.Name`
            Cantidad = $_.Count`
        }`
    } |`
    Sort-Object Grupo |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== MAYORES PAUSAS ENTRE INICIOS DE CICLO ====="
$ordenados = @($ciclos | Sort-Object Unix)
$pausas = for ($i = 1; $i -lt $ordenados.Count; $i++) {`
    $segundos = $ordenados[$i].Unix - $ordenados[$i-1].Unix`
`
    if ($segundos -gt 360) {`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Desde = $ordenados[$i-1].Fecha`
            Hasta = $ordenados[$i].Fecha`

# Reconstruir ciclos y watchers desde los mensajes ya cargados
$ciclosLista = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
$watchersLista = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
foreach ($m in $tg.messages) {`
`
    $txt = ""`
`
    foreach ($p in @($m.text)) {`
        if ($p -is [string]) {`
            $txt += $p`
        }`
        elseif ($null -ne $p.text) {`
            $txt += [string]$p.text`
        }`
    }`
`
    $fecha = [datetime]::ParseExact(`
        $m.date,`
        "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss",`
        [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
    )`
`
    if ($txt -match 'Nuevo ciclo\s*\|\s*(\d{10})') {`
        $epochCapturado = $Matches[1]`
`
        if ($txt -match 'PTB:\s*\$([0-9,]+\.[0-9]{2})') {`
            $ptbCapturado = $Matches[1] -replace ",",""`
`
            $ciclosLista.Add([pscustomobject]@{`
                Hora = $m.date`
                Fecha = $fecha`
                Epoch = [long]$epochCapturado`
                PTB = [double]::Parse(`
                    $ptbCapturado,`
                    [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
                )`
                Watchers = 0`
                HoraWatcher = ""`
            })`
        }`
    }`
`
    if ($txt -match 'PTB watcher aplicado') {`
        if ($txt -match 'vs calculado\s*\$([0-9,]+\.[0-9]{2})') {`
            $ptbCapturado = $Matches[1] -replace ",",""`
`
            $watchersLista.Add([pscustomobject]@{`
                Hora = $m.date`
                Fecha = $fecha`
                PTB = [double]::Parse(`
                    $ptbCapturado,`
                    [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
                )`
            })`
        }`
    }`
}
# Asociar cada watcher con el ciclo anterior más reciente
# que tenga el mismo PTB y esté dentro de los siguientes 300 segundos.
foreach ($w in $watchersLista) {`
`
    $candidato = $ciclosLista |`
        Where-Object {`
            $_.PTB -eq $w.PTB -and`
            $_.Fecha -le $w.Fecha -and`
            ($w.Fecha - $_.Fecha).TotalSeconds -le 300`
        } |`
        Sort-Object Fecha -Descending |`
        Select-Object -First 1`
`
    if ($null -ne $candidato) {`
        $candidato.Watchers++`
        $candidato.HoraWatcher = (`
            $candidato.HoraWatcher + " " + $w.Hora`
        ).Trim()`
    }`
}

$slug = "btc-updown-5m-1793345700"
$needle = "1793345700"
$out = Join-Path $env:TEMP "AUDITORIA_1793345700"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $out | Out-Null
# 1. Registros internos del TAR
$logs = @(`
    "cb_log.jsonl",`
    "flow_log.jsonl",`
    "tw_log.jsonl",`
    "signals_log.jsonl",`
    "bot_output.log",`
    "book_lag.jsonl"`
)
foreach ($log in $logs) {`
    Write-Host "`nBuscando en $log ..." -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    $dest = Join-Path $out $log`
`
    & tar -xOzf $tarFile $log |`
        Select-String -SimpleMatch -Pattern $needle |`
        ForEach-Object { $_.Line } |`
        Set-Content -Path $dest -Encoding UTF8`
`
    $count = (Get-Content $dest | Measure-Object -Line).Lines`
    Write-Host "$log : $count coincidencias"`
}
# 2. Dataflow
Write-Host "`nBuscando en dataflow_log.jsonl..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Select-String -Path (Join-Path $dir "dataflow_log.jsonl") ``
    -SimpleMatch -Pattern $needle |`
    ForEach-Object { $_.Line } |`
    Set-Content (Join-Path $out "dataflow_target.jsonl") -Encoding UTF8
# 3. Microscopio: lectura secuencial de los dos GZIP
foreach ($file in @(`
    "microscopio_20261030_0000.jsonl.gz",`
    "microscopio_20261030_0030.jsonl.gz"`
)) {`
    Write-Host "`nAnalizando $file ..." -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $input = Join-Path $dir $file`
    $dest = Join-Path $out ($file + ".target.jsonl")`
`
    $fs = [IO.File]::OpenRead($input)`
    $gz = [IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $fs, [IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $reader = [IO.StreamReader]::new($gz)`
    $writer = [IO.StreamWriter]::new($dest, $false)`
`
    $count = 0`
    while (($line = $reader.ReadLine()) -ne $null) {`
        if ($line.Contains($needle) -or $line.Contains($slug)) {`
            $writer.WriteLine($line)`
            $count++`
        }`
    }`
`
    $writer.Dispose()`
    $reader.Dispose()`
`
    Write-Host "$file : $count coincidencias"`
}
# 4. Resumen de eventos del microscopio
Write-Host "`n========== TIPOS DE EVENTOS ==========" -ForegroundColor Yellow
Get-ChildItem $out -Filter "*.target.jsonl" | ForEach-Object {`
    Write-Host "`n$($_.Name)" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    Get-Content $_.FullName | ForEach-Object {`
        try {`
            $j = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json`
            "$($j.s) | $($j.e)"`
        } catch {}`
    } | Group-Object | Sort-Object Count -Descending |`
        Select-Object -First 40 Count, Name |`
        Format-Table -AutoSize`
}

# 1. Extraer los registros del TAR
tar -xzf $tarFile -C $extract
Write-Host "`n========== ARCHIVOS EXTRAIDOS ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-ChildItem $extract -File |`
    Select-Object Name, Length |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
# 2. Buscar el epoch exacto en los registros extraídos
Write-Host "`n========== REGISTROS DEL SLUG ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan
foreach ($name in @(`
    "cb_log.jsonl", "flow_log.jsonl", "tw_log.jsonl",`
    "signals_log.jsonl", "bot_output.log", "book_lag.jsonl"`
)) {`
    $path = Join-Path $extract $name`
    if (Test-Path $path) {`
        $matches = Select-String -Path $path -SimpleMatch "$epoch"`
        Write-Host "`n$name : $($matches.Count) coincidencias" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $matches | Select-Object -First 12 | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }`
    }`
}
# 3. Identificar los mercados registrados por el microscopio
foreach ($name in @(`
    "microscopio_20261030_0000.jsonl.gz",`
    "microscopio_20261030_0030.jsonl.gz"`
)) {`
    Write-Host "`n========== $name : METADATOS ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $fs = [IO.File]::OpenRead((Join-Path $dir $name))`
    $gz = [IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $fs, [IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $reader = [IO.StreamReader]::new($gz)`
`
    while (($line = $reader.ReadLine()) -ne $null) {`
        if ($line -match '"s"\s*:\s*"meta"') {`
            try {`
                $j = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
                if ($j.e -eq "mercado" -and $j.d.epoch -eq $epoch) {`
                    $line`
                }`
                elseif ($j.e -eq "resultado") {`
                    $line`
                }`
            } catch {}`
        }`
    }`
`
    $reader.Dispose()`
}

$r.messages | Select-Object -First 10 | Format-List

`

$extract = Join-Path $env:TEMP "AUDITORIA_1793345700\logs_extraidos"
$files = @(`
    @{ Name = "CB";   File = "cb_log.jsonl" },`
    @{ Name = "FLOW"; File = "flow_log.jsonl" },`
    @{ Name = "TW";   File = "tw_log.jsonl" }`
)
$allTrades = foreach ($item in $files) {`
    $path = Join-Path $extract $item.File`
    $records = @(`
        Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
            try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
        }`
    )`
`
    $signals = @{}`
    foreach ($r in $records) {`
        if ($r.kind -like "*_SIGNAL") {`
            $signals[$r.slug] = $r`
        }`
    }`
`
    foreach ($r in $records) {`
        if ($r.kind -like "*_RESULT") {`
            $s = $signals[$r.slug]`
`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                Strategy = $item.Name`
                Slug     = $r.slug`
                Elapsed  = $s.elapsed`
                Left     = if ($null -ne $s.elapsed) { [math]::Round(300 - $s.elapsed, 1) } else { $null }`
                Side     = $r.side`
                Entry    = $r.entry`
                Sigma    = $s.sigma`
                Sigma60  = $s.sigma60`
                Z        = $s.z`
                V60      = $s.v60`
                V10      = $s.v10`
                Vel5     = $s.vel5`
                Ret      = if ($null -ne $s.ret_rel) { $s.ret_rel } else { $s.ret }`
                PMFlow   = $s.pm_net_side_usd`
                CVD_BN   = $s.cvd_bn_60s_side`
                CVD_CB   = $s.cvd_cb_30s_side`
                Won      = $r.won`
                PnL      = $r.pnl_est`
            }`
        }`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n========== RESUMEN HISTORICO ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$allTrades |`
    Group-Object Strategy |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $g = $_.Group`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Strategy = $_.Name`
            Trades   = $g.Count`
            Wins     = @($g | Where-Object Won -eq $true).Count`
            Losses   = @($g | Where-Object Won -eq $false).Count`
            PnL      = [math]::Round((($g | Measure-Object PnL -Sum).Sum), 2)`
        }`
    } | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n========== OPERACIONES COMPLETAS ==========" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$allTrades |`
    Sort-Object Strategy, Elapsed |`
    Format-Table Strategy, Slug, Elapsed, Left, Side, Entry, Sigma, Sigma60, Z, V60, Vel5, Won, PnL -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n========== REGISTROS SIN SENAL EMPAREJADA ==========" -ForegroundColor Yellow
foreach ($item in $files) {`
    $path = Join-Path $extract $item.File`
    $records = @(Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    })`
    $results = @($records | Where-Object { $_.kind -like "*_RESULT" })`
    $missing = @($results | Where-Object { -not ($records | Where-Object {`
        $_.kind -like "*_SIGNAL" -and $_.slug -eq $args[0]`
    }) })`
    # El resumen de operaciones anterior permite detectar emparejamientos faltantes.`
}
# Clasificar operaciones por tiempo transcurrido
$timeReport = foreach ($t in $allTrades) {`
    if ($null -eq $t.Elapsed -or $null -eq $t.PnL) { continue }`
`
    $elapsed = [double]$t.Elapsed`
`
    $bucket = if ($elapsed -lt 120) {`
        "100-119s"`
    } elseif ($elapsed -lt 150) {`
        "120-149s"`
    } elseif ($elapsed -lt 180) {`
        "150-179s"`
    } else {`
        "180s o mas"`
    }`
`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Strategy = $t.Strategy`
        Bucket   = $bucket`
        Won      = $t.Won`
        PnL      = [double]$t.PnL`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n===== RESULTADOS POR TIEMPO =====" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$timeReport |`
    Group-Object Strategy, Bucket |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $g = $_.Group`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Grupo  = $_.Name`
            Trades = $g.Count`
            Wins   = @($g | Where-Object Won -eq $true).Count`
            Losses = @($g | Where-Object Won -eq $false).Count`
            WinPct = [math]::Round(`
                100 * (@($g | Where-Object Won -eq $true).Count) / $g.Count, 2`
            )`
            PnL = [math]::Round(($g | Measure-Object PnL -Sum).Sum, 2)`
        }`
    } | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== PERDIDAS HISTORICAS =====" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$allTrades |`
    Where-Object { $_.Won -eq $false } |`
    Sort-Object PnL |`
    Format-Table Strategy, Slug, Elapsed, Left, Entry, Sigma, Sigma60, Z, V60, Vel5, PnL -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== COBERTURA DE DATOS =====" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$allTrades |`
    Group-Object Strategy |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $g = $_.Group`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Strategy       = $_.Name`
            Total          = $g.Count`
            ConTiempo      = @($g | Where-Object { $null -ne $_.Elapsed }).Count`
            ConSigma       = @($g | Where-Object { $null -ne $_.Sigma }).Count`
            ConSigma60     = @($g | Where-Object { $null -ne $_.Sigma60 }).Count`
            ConVelocidad   = @($g | Where-Object { $null -ne $_.V60 }).Count`
        }`
    } | Format-Table -AutoSize

    @{ Name = "CB";   File = "cb_log.jsonl" },`

    @{ Name = "TW";   File = "tw_log.jsonl" },`
    @{ Name = "LATE"; File = "late_log.jsonl" }`
)
Write-Host "`n===== RESUMEN POR ESTRATEGIA =====" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$allTrades | Group-Object Strategy | ForEach-Object {`
    $g = $_.Group`
    $valid = @($g | Where-Object { $_.HasPnL -and $_.Won -is [bool] })`
`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Strategy      = $_.Name`
        Results       = $g.Count`
        WithSignal    = @($g | Where-Object HasSignal).Count`
        WithPnL       = @($g | Where-Object HasPnL).Count`
        Wins          = @($valid | Where-Object Won).Count`
        Losses        = @($valid | Where-Object { !$_.Won }).Count`
        PnL           = [math]::Round(`
            ($valid | Measure-Object PnL -Sum).Sum, 2`
        )`
    }`
} | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== TODAS LAS PERDIDAS VALIDAS =====" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$allTrades |`
    Where-Object { $_.HasPnL -and $_.Won -eq $false } |`
    Sort-Object PnL |`
    Format-Table Strategy, Slug, Elapsed, Left, Entry, Sigma, Sigma60, Z, V60, Vel5, PnL -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== REGISTROS SIN SEÑAL O SIN PNL =====" -ForegroundColor Yellow
$allTrades |`
    Where-Object { !$_.HasSignal -or !$_.HasPnL } |`
    Format-Table Strategy, Slug, HasSignal, HasPnL, Won, PnL -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n===== OPERACIONES POR TIEMPO =====" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$allTrades |`
    Where-Object { $_.HasPnL -and $null -ne $_.Elapsed } |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $e = [double]$_.Elapsed`
        $bucket = if ($e -lt 120) { "100-119s" }`
            elseif ($e -lt 150) { "120-149s" }`
            elseif ($e -lt 180) { "150-179s" }`
            else { "180s o mas" }`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Strategy = $_.Strategy`
            Bucket = $bucket`
            Won = $_.Won`
            PnL = [double]$_.PnL`
        }`
    } |`
    Group-Object Strategy, Bucket |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $g = $_.Group`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Grupo = $_.Name`
            Trades = $g.Count`
            Wins = @($g | Where-Object Won).Count`
            Losses = @($g | Where-Object { !$_.Won }).Count`
            PnL = [math]::Round(($g | Measure-Object PnL -Sum).Sum, 2)`
        }`
    } | Format-Table -AutoSize

foreach ($name in @("late_log.jsonl","cb_log.jsonl","flow_log.jsonl","tw_log.jsonl")) {`
`
    $path = Join-Path $extract $name`
    $records = @(Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    })`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $name ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $records |`
        Group-Object kind |`
        Select-Object Name, Count |`
        Format-Table -AutoSize`
`
    Write-Host "`n--- EJEMPLOS DE SIGNAL ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
    $records |`
        Where-Object { $_.kind -like "*_SIGNAL" } |`
        Select-Object -First 2 |`
        ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8`
`
    Write-Host "`n--- EJEMPLOS DE RESULT ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
    $records |`
        Where-Object { $_.kind -like "*_RESULT" } |`
        Select-Object -First 2 |`
        ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8`
}
$extract = Join-Path $env:TEMP "AUDITORIA_1793345700\logs_extraidos"
foreach ($name in @("late_log.jsonl","cb_log.jsonl","flow_log.jsonl","tw_log.jsonl")) {`
`
    $path = Join-Path $extract $name`
    $records = @(Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    })`
`
    $signals = @($records | Where-Object { $_.kind -like "*_SIGNAL" })`
    $results = @($records | Where-Object { $_.kind -like "*_RESULT" })`
    $executed = @($signals | Where-Object { $_.ok -eq $true })`
    $failed = @($signals | Where-Object { $_.ok -eq $false })`
`
    $duplicateEntries = @(`
        $executed | Group-Object slug | Where-Object { $_.Count -gt 1 }`
    )`
`
    $duplicateResults = @(`
        $results | Group-Object slug | Where-Object { $_.Count -gt 1 }`
    )`
`
    $unmatched = @(`
        $results | Where-Object {`
            $result = $_`
            -not ($executed | Where-Object {`
                $_.slug -eq $result.slug -and $_.ts -le $result.ts`
            } | Select-Object -First 1)`
        }`
    )`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $name ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Signals          = $signals.Count`
        ExecutedOK       = $executed.Count`
        FailedOKFalse    = $failed.Count`
        Results          = $results.Count`
        UniqueExecuted   = @($executed.slug | Sort-Object -Unique).Count`
        UniqueResults    = @($results.slug | Sort-Object -Unique).Count`
        MultipleEntries  = $duplicateEntries.Count`
        MultipleResults  = $duplicateResults.Count`
        ResultsUnmatched = $unmatched.Count`
    } | Format-List`
`
    if ($failed.Count -gt 0) {`
        Write-Host "--- EJEMPLOS DE INTENTOS FALLIDOS ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $failed | Select-Object -First 3 slug,ts,elapsed,side,ok,try,resp |`
            Format-List`
    }`
`
    if ($duplicateEntries.Count -gt 0) {`
        Write-Host "--- MERCADOS CON VARIAS ENTRADAS EJECUTADAS ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $duplicateEntries | Select-Object -First 10 Name,Count |`
            Format-Table -AutoSize`
    }`
`
    if ($unmatched.Count -gt 0) {`
        Write-Host "--- RESULTADOS SIN SEÑAL EJECUTADA PREVIA ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $unmatched | Select-Object -First 5 slug,ts,side,winner,entry,pnl_est |`
            Format-Table -AutoSize`
    }`
}
$extract = Join-Path $env:TEMP "AUDITORIA_1793345700\logs_extraidos"
foreach ($prefix in @("CB","FLOW","TW","LATE")) {`
`
    $path = Join-Path $extract ($prefix.ToLower() + "_log.jsonl")`
`
    $records = @(Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    })`
`
    $signals = @($records | Where-Object {`
        $_.kind -eq "${prefix}_SIGNAL" -and $_.ok -eq $true`
    })`
`
    $results = @($records | Where-Object {`
        $_.kind -eq "${prefix}_RESULT"`
    })`
`
    $missing = @($signals | Where-Object {`
        $signal = $_`
        -not ($results | Where-Object {`
            $_.slug -eq $signal.slug -and $_.ts -gt $signal.ts`
        } | Select-Object -First 1)`
    })`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $prefix ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        EntradasEjecutadas = $signals.Count`
        ResultadosReales   = $results.Count`
        SinResultado       = $missing.Count`
    } | Format-List`
`
    if ($missing.Count -gt 0) {`
        Write-Host "--- ENTRADAS SIN RESULTADO ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $missing | Select-Object slug,ts,elapsed,side,real_ask,ask,fill_price,amount,usdc |`
            Format-Table -AutoSize`
    }`
}

$extract = Join-Path $env:TEMP "AUDITORIA_1793345700\logs_extraidos"
$allTrades = @()
foreach ($prefix in @("CB","FLOW","TW","LATE")) {`
`
    $path = Join-Path $extract ($prefix.ToLower() + "_log.jsonl")`
`
    $records = @(Get-Content $path | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    })`
`
    $signals = @($records | Where-Object {`
        $_.kind -eq "${prefix}_SIGNAL" -and $_.ok -eq $true`
    })`
`
    $results = @($records | Where-Object {`
        $_.kind -eq "${prefix}_RESULT"`
    })`
`
    foreach ($result in $results) {`
`
        $signal = $signals |`
            Where-Object {`
                $_.slug -eq $result.slug -and $_.ts -lt $result.ts`
            } |`
            Sort-Object ts -Descending |`
            Select-Object -First 1`
`
        if ($null -eq $signal) { continue }`
`
        $entry = $result.entry`
        if ($null -eq $entry) { $entry = $signal.fill_price }`
        if ($null -eq $entry) { $entry = $signal.real_ask }`
        if ($null -eq $entry) { $entry = $signal.ask }`
`
        $allTrades += [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Strategy = $prefix`
            Slug     = $result.slug`
            Side     = $result.side`
            Elapsed  = $signal.elapsed`
            Entry    = $entry`
            Won      = $result.won`
            PnL      = $result.pnl_est`
            Sigma    = $signal.sigma`
            Sigma60  = $signal.sigma60`
            Z        = $signal.z`
            V60      = $signal.v60`
            V10      = $signal.v10`
            Vel5     = $signal.vel5`
            Ret      = $signal.ret`
            RetRel   = $signal.ret_rel`
            PMFlow   = $signal.pm_net_side_usd`
            CVD_BN   = $signal.cvd_bn_60s_side`
            CVD_CB   = $signal.cvd_cb_30s_side`
            DSDelta  = $signal.ds_delta`
            DSAge    = $signal.ds_age_s`
            Top3     = $signal.real_top3`
        }`
    }`
}

$metrics = @(`
    "Elapsed","Entry","Sigma","Sigma60","Z","V60","V10",`

`
    Write-Host "`n--- $prefix ---" -ForegroundColor Green`
`
    $group = @($allTrades | Where-Object Strategy -eq $prefix)`
`
    foreach ($metric in $metrics) {`
`
        $wins = @($group | Where-Object Won | ForEach-Object {`
            $value = $_.$metric`
            if ($null -ne $value -and "$value" -ne "") {`
                try { [double]$value } catch {}`
            }`
        } | Sort-Object)`
`
        $losses = @($group | Where-Object { -not $_.Won } | ForEach-Object {`
            $value = $_.$metric`
            if ($null -ne $value -and "$value" -ne "") {`
                try { [double]$value } catch {}`
            }`
        } | Sort-Object)`
`
        if ($wins.Count -eq 0 -or $losses.Count -eq 0) { continue }`
`
        $winMedian = $wins[[math]::Floor($wins.Count / 2)]`
        $lossMedian = $losses[[math]::Floor($losses.Count / 2)]`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Metric       = $metric`
            WinnersN     = $wins.Count`
            WinnersMed   = [math]::Round($winMedian,4)`
            WinnersMin   = [math]::Round($wins[0],4)`
            WinnersMax   = [math]::Round($wins[-1],4)`
            LosersN      = $losses.Count`
            LosersMed    = [math]::Round($lossMedian,4)`
            LosersMin    = [math]::Round($losses[0],4)`
            LosersMax    = [math]::Round($losses[-1],4)`
        }`
    } | Format-Table -AutoSize`
}
$metrics = @(`
    "Elapsed","Entry","Sigma","Sigma60","Z","V60","V10",`
    "Vel5","Ret","RetRel","PMFlow","CVD_BN","CVD_CB",`
    "DSDelta","DSAge","Top3"`

foreach ($prefix in @("CB","FLOW","TW","LATE")) {`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $prefix ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $group = @($allTrades | Where-Object { $_.Strategy -eq $prefix })`
`
    $comparison = foreach ($metric in $metrics) {`
`
        $wins = @(`
            $group | Where-Object { $_.Won -eq $true } |`
            ForEach-Object {`
                $value = $_.$metric`
                if ($null -ne $value -and "$value" -ne "") {`
                    try { [double]$value } catch {}`
                }`
            } | Sort-Object`
        )`
`
        $losses = @(`
            $group | Where-Object { $_.Won -eq $false } |`
            ForEach-Object {`
                $value = $_.$metric`
                if ($null -ne $value -and "$value" -ne "") {`
                    try { [double]$value } catch {}`
                }`
            } | Sort-Object`
        )`
`
        if ($wins.Count -eq 0 -or $losses.Count -eq 0) {`
            continue`
        }`
`
        $winMedian = $wins[[math]::Floor($wins.Count / 2)]`
        $lossMedian = $losses[[math]::Floor($losses.Count / 2)]`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Metric     = $metric`
            WinnersN   = $wins.Count`
            WinnersMed = [math]::Round($winMedian,4)`
            WinnersMin = [math]::Round($wins[0],4)`
            WinnersMax = [math]::Round($wins[-1],4)`
            LosersN    = $losses.Count`
            LosersMed  = [math]::Round($lossMedian,4)`
            LosersMin  = [math]::Round($losses[0],4)`
            LosersMax  = [math]::Round($losses[-1],4)`
        }`
    }`
`
    $comparison | Format-Table -AutoSize`
}

$rules = @(`
    # CB`
    @{ Strategy="CB"; Name="Vel5 >= 5"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.Vel5 -and [double]$t.Vel5 -ge 5 } }`
    @{ Strategy="CB"; Name="PMFlow >= 4000"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.PMFlow -and [double]$t.PMFlow -ge 4000 } }`
    @{ Strategy="CB"; Name="DSDelta > 0"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.DSDelta -and [double]$t.DSDelta -gt 0 } }`
    @{ Strategy="CB"; Name="Vel5 >= 5 AND DSDelta > 0"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.Vel5 -and $null -ne $t.DSDelta -and [double]$t.Vel5 -ge 5 -and [double]$t.DSDelta -gt 0 } }`
`
    # FLOW`
    @{ Strategy="FLOW"; Name="RetRel >= 0.10"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.RetRel -and [double]$t.RetRel -ge 0.10 } }`
    @{ Strategy="FLOW"; Name="RetRel >= 0.15"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.RetRel -and [double]$t.RetRel -ge 0.15 } }`
    @{ Strategy="FLOW"; Name="V60 <= 15"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.V60 -and [double]$t.V60 -le 15 } }`
    @{ Strategy="FLOW"; Name="RetRel >= 0.10 AND V60 <= 15"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.RetRel -and $null -ne $t.V60 -and [double]$t.RetRel -ge 0.10 -and [double]$t.V60 -le 15 } }`
`
    # TW`
    @{ Strategy="TW"; Name="Sigma >= 2.5"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.Sigma -and [double]$t.Sigma -ge 2.5 } }`
    @{ Strategy="TW"; Name="Sigma >= 3"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.Sigma -and [double]$t.Sigma -ge 3 } }`
    @{ Strategy="TW"; Name="V10 >= 25"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.V10 -and [double]$t.V10 -ge 25 } }`
    @{ Strategy="TW"; Name="Sigma >= 2.5 AND V10 >= 25"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.Sigma -and $null -ne $t.V10 -and [double]$t.Sigma -ge 2.5 -and [double]$t.V10 -ge 25 } }`
`
    # LATE`
    @{ Strategy="LATE"; Name="PMFlow >= 3000"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.PMFlow -and [double]$t.PMFlow -ge 3000 } }`
    @{ Strategy="LATE"; Name="PMFlow >= 4000"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.PMFlow -and [double]$t.PMFlow -ge 4000 } }`
    @{ Strategy="LATE"; Name="CVD_CB >= 1"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.CVD_CB -and [double]$t.CVD_CB -ge 1 } }`
    @{ Strategy="LATE"; Name="PMFlow >= 3000 AND CVD_CB >= 1"; Test={ param($t) $null -ne $t.PMFlow -and $null -ne $t.CVD_CB -and [double]$t.PMFlow -ge 3000 -and [double]$t.CVD_CB -ge 1 } }`
)
$results = foreach ($strategy in @("CB","FLOW","TW","LATE")) {`
`
    $group = @($allTrades | Where-Object { $_.Strategy -eq $strategy })`
`
    $baselinePnL = ($group | Measure-Object -Property PnL -Sum).Sum`
`
    foreach ($rule in @($rules | Where-Object { $_.Strategy -eq $strategy })) {`
`
        $blocked = @($group | Where-Object {`
            $trade = $_`
            & $rule.Test $trade`
        })`
`
        $blockedWins = @($blocked | Where-Object { $_.Won -eq $true })`
        $blockedLosses = @($blocked | Where-Object { $_.Won -eq $false })`
`
        $blockedPnL = ($blocked | Measure-Object -Property PnL -Sum).Sum`
        $avoidedLoss = -1 * (($blockedLosses | Measure-Object -Property PnL -Sum).Sum)`
        $sacrificedProfit = ($blockedWins | Measure-Object -Property PnL -Sum).Sum`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            Strategy       = $strategy`
            Rule           = $rule.Name`
            Blocked        = $blocked.Count`
            BlockedWins    = $blockedWins.Count`
            BlockedLosses  = $blockedLosses.Count`
            AvoidedLoss    = [math]::Round($avoidedLoss,2)`
            SacrificedWin  = [math]::Round($sacrificedProfit,2)`
            BaselinePnL    = [math]::Round($baselinePnL,2)`
            NewPnL         = [math]::Round(($baselinePnL - $blockedPnL),2)`
            Improvement    = [math]::Round((-$blockedPnL),2)`
        }`
    }`
}
$results |`
    Sort-Object Strategy, Improvement -Descending |`
    Format-Table Strategy,Rule,Blocked,BlockedWins,BlockedLosses,AvoidedLoss,SacrificedWin,BaselinePnL,NewPnL,Improvement -AutoSize

function Show-Range($start, $end) {`
    Write-Host "`n========== LINEAS $start - $end ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    for ($i = $start; $i -le $end; $i++) {`
        "{0,5}: {1}" -f $i, $lines[$i - 1]`
    }`
}

@'`
import os, gzip, json, tarfile`
`
EPOCH = 1793345400`
SLUG = f"btc-updown-5m-{EPOCH}"`
OUT = "extracto_1793345400"`
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)`
`
def write_gz(path, records):`
    n = 0`
    with gzip.open(path, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as out:`
        for line in records:`
            out.write(line)`
            if not line.endswith("\n"):`
                out.write("\n")`
            n += 1`
    return n`
`
# 1) Microscopio: todos los eventos de los cinco minutos del mercado`
micro_files = [`
    "microscopio_20261030_0000.jsonl.gz",`
    "microscopio_20261030_0030.jsonl.gz"`
]`
micro_out = os.path.join(OUT, "microscopio_ciclo_completo.jsonl.gz")`
count = 0`
`
with gzip.open(micro_out, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as out:`
    for fn in micro_files:`
        if not os.path.exists(fn):`
            continue`
        with gzip.open(fn, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as src:`
            for line in src:`
                try:`
                    d = json.loads(line)`
                    t = float(d.get("t", 0))`
                    if EPOCH <= t < EPOCH + 300:`
                        out.write(line)`
                        count += 1`
                except Exception:`
                    pass`
`
print("MICROSCOPIO: eventos del ciclo:", count)`
`
# 2) DataFlow: registros que mencionan directamente el mercado`
df = "dataflow_log.jsonl"`
df_out = os.path.join(OUT, "dataflow_mercado.txt")`
hits = 0`
`
with open(df, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as src, \`
     open(df_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:`
    for line in src:`
        if str(EPOCH) in line or SLUG in line:`
            out.write(line)`
            hits += 1`
`
print("DATAFLOW: coincidencias directas:", hits)`
`
# 3) Logs de compras: buscar el mercado dentro del TAR.GZ`
tar_path = "logs_1030_0808.tar.gz"`
tar_out = os.path.join(OUT, "compras_mercado.txt")`
tar_hits = 0`
`
if os.path.exists(tar_path):`
    with tarfile.open(tar_path, "r:gz") as tar, open(tar_out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:`
        print("\nARCHIVOS DENTRO DEL TAR:")`
        for member in tar.getmembers():`
            print(member.name, member.size)`
            if not member.isfile():`
                continue`
            f = tar.extractfile(member)`
            if f is None:`
                continue`
            for raw in f:`
                line = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")`
                if str(EPOCH) in line or SLUG in line:`
                    out.write(f"\n--- {member.name} ---\n")`
                    out.write(line)`
                    tar_hits += 1`
`
print("\nLOGS: coincidencias directas:", tar_hits)`
print("\nExtractos guardados en:", os.path.abspath(OUT))`

@'`
import os, json, gzip, tarfile`
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta`
from collections import Counter`
`
EPOCH = 1793345400`
END = EPOCH + 300`
TZ = timezone(timedelta(hours=-7))`
`
def hora(ts):`
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts), TZ).strftime("%H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3]`
`
print("=== CICLO ===")`
print("Inicio local:", hora(EPOCH))`
print("Fin local:   ", hora(END))`
`
# 1. Registros del bot dentro del TAR`
print("\n=== COMPRAS Y SEÑALES DEL BOT ===")`
with tarfile.open("logs_1030_0808.tar.gz", "r:gz") as tar:`
    for name in ["cb_log.jsonl", "flow_log.jsonl", "tw_log.jsonl",`
                 "signals_log.jsonl", "bot_output.log"]:`
        m = next((x for x in tar.getmembers() if x.name == name), None)`
        if not m:`
            continue`
        f = tar.extractfile(m)`
        matches = []`
        for raw in f:`
            line = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()`
            if str(EPOCH) in line or f"btc-updown-5m-{EPOCH}" in line:`
                matches.append(line)`
        print(f"\n--- {name}: {len(matches)} coincidencias ---")`
        for line in matches[:35]:`
            print(line[:1800])`
`
# 2. DataFlow: registros del mercado`
print("\n=== DATAFLOW ===")`
path = os.path.join("extracto_1793345400", "dataflow_mercado.txt")`
with open(path, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:`
    lines = f.readlines()`
for line in lines[:25]:`
    print(line[:2500].rstrip())`
if len(lines) > 25:`
    print(f"... quedan {len(lines)-25} registros")`
`
# 3. Microscopio: distribución de eventos`
print("\n=== MICROSCOPIO: FUENTES Y TIPOS ===")`
path = os.path.join("extracto_1793345400", "microscopio_ciclo_completo.jsonl.gz")`
counts = Counter()`
first = {}`
last = {}`
meta = []`
`
with gzip.open(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as f:`
    for line in f:`
        try:`
            d = json.loads(line)`
            s, e = d.get("s"), d.get("e")`
            counts[(s,e)] += 1`
            first.setdefault((s,e), d.get("t"))`
            last[(s,e)] = d.get("t")`
            if s == "meta" and e in ("mercado", "resultado"):`
                meta.append(d)`
        except Exception:`
            pass`
`
for (s,e), n in counts.most_common(50):`
    print(f"{s:8} {e:18} {n:8} | {hora(first[(s,e)])} - {hora(last[(s,e)])}")`
`
print("\n=== METADATOS DEL MERCADO ===")`
for d in meta:`
    print(json.dumps(d, ensure_ascii=False)[:2500])`

@'`
import json`
import re`
from collections import Counter`
`
with open("result.json", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:`
    data = json.load(f)`
`
msgs = data.get("messages", [])`
`
pat = re.compile(`
    r'\b(CB|FLOW|TW)\b|'`
    r'WINNER|LOSER|WON|LOST|'`
    r'GANAD|PERD|PNL|P&L|'`
    r'COMPRA|BUY|ENTRY|ENTRADA|'`
    r'UP|DOWN|DN|'`
    r'1793\d{6}',`
    re.I`
)`
`
hits = []`
for m in msgs:`
    txt = m.get("text", "")`
    if isinstance(txt, list):`
        txt = " ".join(str(x) for x in txt)`
    if pat.search(str(txt)):`
        hits.append(m)`
`
print("TOTAL MENSAJES:", len(msgs))`
print("MENSAJES COINCIDENTES:", len(hits))`
`
print("\n--- DISTRIBUCION POR REMITENTE ---")`
print(Counter(m.get("from", "?") for m in hits))`
`
print("\n--- MENSAJES COINCIDENTES (máximo 100) ---")`
for m in hits[:100]:`
    print(`
        "\n[" + str(m.get("date", "")) + "]",`
        str(m.get("from", "")),`
        "\n",`
        str(m.get("text", ""))[:1200]`
    )`

'@ | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 .\listar_tar.py
python .\listar_tar.py
python .\inspeccionar_logs_tar.py
@'`
import tarfile`
import json`
import csv`
import re`
from collections import Counter, defaultdict`
`
TAR = "logs_1030_0808.tar.gz"`
`
with open("operaciones_cb_flow_tw.csv", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:`
    ops = list(csv.DictReader(f))`
`
by_slug = defaultdict(list)`
for op in ops:`
    slug = "btc-updown-5m-" + op["market"]`
    by_slug[slug].append(op)`
`
targets = [`
    "tw_log.jsonl",`
    "flow_log.jsonl",`
    "cb_log.jsonl",`
    "signals_log.jsonl",`
    "book_lag.jsonl",`
    "bot_output.log"`
]`
`
results = defaultdict(Counter)`
examples = defaultdict(list)`
pre_entry = defaultdict(Counter)`
bot_examples = defaultdict(list)`
`
with tarfile.open(TAR, "r:gz") as tar:`
    for name in targets:`
        print("\nProcesando:", name)`
        f = tar.extractfile(name)`
`
        for raw in f:`
            line = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()`
            if not line:`
                continue`
`
            if name == "bot_output.log":`
                # Solo coincidencias exactas de slugs conocidos.`
                for slug, market_ops in by_slug.items():`
                    if slug not in line:`
                        continue`
`
                    results[name][slug] += 1`
                    if len(bot_examples[slug]) < 3:`
                        bot_examples[slug].append(line[:500])`
                continue`
`
            try:`
                obj = json.loads(line)`
            except:`
                continue`
`
            if not isinstance(obj, dict):`
                continue`
`
            slug = obj.get("slug")`
            if slug not in by_slug:`
                continue`
`
            results[name][slug] += 1`
            kind = str(obj.get("kind", obj.get("decision", "SIN_TIPO")))`
            results[name][kind] += 1`
`
            if len(examples[(name, slug)]) < 3:`
                examples[(name, slug)].append(obj)`
`
            ts = obj.get("ts")`
            if ts is None:`
                continue`
`
            for op in by_slug[slug]:`
                entry_ts = float(op["entry_unix"] or 0)`
                if not entry_ts:`
                    entry_ts = float(op["market"]) + float(op["elapsed_s"] or 0)`
`
                if entry_ts - 30 <= float(ts) <= entry_ts:`
                    pre_entry[(name, op["strategy"])][kind] += 1`
`
print("\n" + "=" * 95)`
print("COBERTURA EXACTA POR ARCHIVO")`
print("=" * 95)`
`
for name in targets:`
    covered = sum(1 for slug in by_slug if results[name][slug] > 0)`
    total_records = sum(results[name][slug] for slug in by_slug)`
    print(f"{name}: {covered}/{len(by_slug)} mercados | {total_records:,} registros")`
`
print("\n" + "=" * 95)`
print("OPERACIONES CON REGISTROS EN LOGS DE ESTRATEGIA")`
print("=" * 95)`
`
for slug, market_ops in sorted(by_slug.items()):`
    found = []`
    for name in ("cb_log.jsonl", "flow_log.jsonl", "tw_log.jsonl"):`
        if results[name][slug]:`
            found.append(f"{name.replace('_log.jsonl','')}={results[name][slug]}")`
`
    if found:`
        print(slug, "|", "; ".join(found))`
`
print("\n" + "=" * 95)`
print("TIPOS DE REGISTRO EN VENTANA PREVIA A LA ENTRADA")`
print("=" * 95)`
`
for (name, strategy), counts in sorted(pre_entry.items()):`
    print("\n", name, "|", strategy)`
    for kind, count in counts.most_common(15):`
        print(" ", kind, count)`
`
print("\n" + "=" * 95)`
print("EJEMPLOS DE REGISTROS: MERCADOS YA ESTUDIADOS")`
print("=" * 95)`
`
for slug in (`
    "btc-updown-5m-1793344200",`
    "btc-updown-5m-1793344500",`
    "btc-updown-5m-1793345400"`
):`
    print("\nMERCADO:", slug)`
    for name in ("cb_log.jsonl", "flow_log.jsonl", "tw_log.jsonl"):`
        for obj in examples.get((name, slug), []):`
            print(name, json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)[:1300])`
`
    for line in bot_examples.get(slug, []):`
        print("BOT:", line)`
`
print("\nFIN DEL CRUCE")`

@'`
import tarfile, csv, re`
from collections import defaultdict`
`
TAR = "logs_1030_0808.tar.gz"`
CSV = "operaciones_cb_flow_tw.csv"`
CORTE = 1793301000`
TARGET = "1793345400"`
`
with open(CSV, encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:`
    ops = list(csv.DictReader(f))`
`
selected = [`
    o for o in ops`
    if o["strategy"].upper() in ("CB","FLOW","TW")`
    and int(o["market"]) > CORTE`
    and (o["market"] == TARGET or`
         (o["outcome"].upper() == "WON" and`
          int(o["market"]) in {`
              1793340000,1793340300,1793342700,`
              1793343300,1793344200,1793344500`
          }))`
]`
`
targets = defaultdict(list)`
for o in selected:`
    market = o["market"]`
    entry = float(o["entry_unix"])`
    elapsed = entry - float(market)`
    targets["btc-updown-5m-" + market].append(`
        (o["strategy"].upper(), elapsed, o["outcome"], o["side_buy"])`
    )`
`
output = []`
`
with tarfile.open(TAR, "r:gz") as tar:`
    f = tar.extractfile("bot_output.log")`
`
    for raw in f:`
        line = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").rstrip()`
`
        for slug, entries in targets.items():`
            if slug not in line:`
                continue`
`
            match = re.search(r"elapsed=(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)s", line)`
            if match:`
                elapsed = float(match.group(1))`
                if any(e-40 <= elapsed <= e+5`
                       for _,e,_,_ in entries):`
                    output.append(line)`
            elif any(word in line for word in`
                     ("SIGNAL","BUY","SKIP","DIAG","FLOW","CB ","TW ")):`
                output.append(line)`
`
with open("ventanas_entrada_bot.txt","w",encoding="utf-8") as f:`
    f.write("\n".join(output))`
`
print("Ventanas extraídas:",len(output))`
print("Archivo: ventanas_entrada_bot.txt")`
for slug, entries in targets.items():`
    print("\n",slug)`
    for strategy, elapsed, outcome, side in entries:`
        print(strategy,side,outcome,"entrada t+",round(elapsed,2))`
'@ | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 .\extraer_ventanas_entrada.py
python .\extraer_ventanas_entrada.py
python .\ver_snapshots_comparacion.py
import csv, json, re
from collections import defaultdict
DATA = "dataflow_log.jsonl"
OPS = "operaciones_cb_flow_tw.csv"
# Mercados posteriores al corte y controles seleccionados
MARKETS = {`
    1793340000, 1793340300, 1793342700,`
    1793343300, 1793344200, 1793344500,`
    1793345400`
}
STRATEGIES = {"CB", "FLOW", "TW"}
# Cargar operaciones exactas
entries = []
with open(OPS, newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f):
        strategy = r.get("strategy", "").upper()
        if strategy not in STRATEGIES:
            continue
        raw_market = str(r.get("market", ""))
        match = re.search(r"(\d{10})", raw_market)
        if not match:
            continue
        epoch = int(match.group(1))
        if epoch not in MARKETS:
            continue
        try:
            entry_ts = float(r["entry_unix"])
            continue
        entries.append({`
            "epoch": epoch,`
            "strategy": strategy,`
            "side": r.get("side_buy", ""),`
            "entry_ts": entry_ts,`
            "pnl": r.get("pnl", ""),`
            "price": r.get("entry_price", "")`
        })
# Agrupar entradas por mercado
by_market = defaultdict(list)
for e in entries:
    by_market[e["epoch"]].append(e)
# Leer snapshots
snapshots = defaultdict(list)
with open(DATA, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for line in f:
        try:
            o = json.loads(line)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if o.get("kind") != "snapshot":
            continue
        epoch = o.get("epoch")
        if epoch is None:
            continue
        try:
            epoch = int(float(epoch))
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            continue
        if epoch in MARKETS:
            snapshots[epoch].append(o)
FIELDS = [
    "elapsed", "btc_from_open", "vel_15s", "vel_30s", "vel_60s",`
    "accel", "cvd_30s", "cvd_perp_30s", "cvd_cb_30s",`
    "book_imb5_now", "book_imb_30s", "cb_premium_chg_30s",`
    "taker_buysell_ratio", "basis_usd", "ds_delta", "ds_vel_10s",`
    "ds_vel_30s", "ds_age_s", "cl_delta", "cl_age_s",`
    "pm_up_mid", "pm_fav_flips", "pm_fav_age_s",`
    "pm_flow_net60_usd", "pm_ws_net60_usd",`
    "pm_book_age_s", "tw_sigma", "tw_v10", "tw_z", "regime"
]
for epoch in sorted(by_market):
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print("MERCADO", epoch, "| snapshots:", len(snapshots[epoch]))
        print(`
            f"\n{e['strategy']} {e['side']} | entrada={e['entry_ts']:.2f}"`
            f" | precio={e['price']} | PnL={e['pnl']}"
        )
        window = [
            s for s in snapshots[epoch]
            if e["entry_ts"] - 30 <= float(s.get("ts", 0)) <= e["entry_ts"]
        ]
        if not window:
            print("  SIN SNAPSHOTS EN LOS 30s PREVIOS")
            continue
        for s in window:
            vals = " | ".join(`
                f"{k}={s.get(k)}" for k in FIELDS
                if k in s
            )
            print(f"  t={s.get('ts')}: {vals}")

    Write-Host "Total registros válidos: $($registros.Count)"`
    Write-Host "Errores de lectura: $errores"`
`
    if ($registros.Count -eq 0) {`
        continue`
    }`
`
    $fechas = $registros | Sort-Object ts`
`
    Write-Host "Primer registro:"`
    [DateTimeOffset]::FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(`
        [long]($fechas[0].ts * 1000)`
    ).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")`
`
    Write-Host "Último registro:"`
    [DateTimeOffset]::FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(`
        [long]($fechas[-1].ts * 1000)`
    ).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")`
`
    $posteriores = @(`
        $registros | Where-Object { $_.ts -ge $corte }`
    )`
`
    Write-Host "`nREGISTROS DESDE LAS 13:40: $($posteriores.Count)"`
`
    Write-Host "`nDESGLOSE POR KIND:"`
    $posteriores |`
        Group-Object kind |`
        Sort-Object Count -Descending |`
        Select-Object Name, Count |`
        Format-Table -AutoSize`
`
    if ($archivo -like "*post_loss_guard*") {`
        Write-Host "`nDESGLOSE POR ESTRATEGIA Y DECISION:"`
        $posteriores |`
            Group-Object strategy, reason, blocked |`
            Select-Object Name, Count |`
            Format-Table -AutoSize`
    }`
}

@'`
import json`
import re`
from pathlib import Path`
`
BASE = Path(r"C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta")`
data = json.loads((BASE / "result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))`
`
def flatten(v):`
    if isinstance(v, str):`
        return v`
    if isinstance(v, list):`
        return "".join(flatten(x) for x in v)`
    if isinstance(v, dict):`
        return flatten(v.get("text", "")) if "text" in v else ""`
    return ""`
`
ranges = [(142650, 142740), (143490, 143540)]`
`
for lo, hi in ranges:`
    print(f"\n========== MENSAJES {lo} A {hi} ==========")`
    for m in data.get("messages", []):`
        mid = m.get("id")`
        if not isinstance(mid, int) or not lo <= mid <= hi:`
            continue`
`
        txt = flatten(m.get("text", "")).replace("\n", " ")`
        if len(txt) > 700:`
            txt = txt[:700] + "..."`
`
        print(`
            f"ID={mid} | FECHA={m.get('date')} | "`
            f"TIPO={m.get('type')} | {txt}"`
        )`
'@ | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $base "secuencia_ptb.py") -Encoding UTF8

@'`
import json`
import csv`
from pathlib import Path`
from collections import Counter, defaultdict`
`
base = Path(r"C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta")`
ptb_csv = base / "ptb_sesiones_tw94.csv"`
dataflow = base / "dataflow_log.jsonl"`
`
targets = set()`
`
with ptb_csv.open("r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:`
    for row in csv.DictReader(f):`
        if row.get("tipo_ciclo") == "OBJETIVO_TW":`
            targets.add(str(int(float(row["epoch"]))))`
`
counts = Counter()`
samples = defaultdict(list)`
matched_epochs = set()`
max_samples = 3`
`
with dataflow.open("r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:`
    for line_no, line in enumerate(f, 1):`
        hits = [e for e in targets if e in line]`
        if not hits:`
            continue`
`
        try:`
            obj = json.loads(line)`
        except Exception:`
            continue`
`
        if not isinstance(obj, dict):`
            continue`
`
        kind = str(`
            obj.get("kind") or obj.get("event")`
            or obj.get("type") or obj.get("e")`
            or obj.get("s") or "SIN_TIPO"`
        )`
`
        counts[kind] += 1`
        matched_epochs.update(hits)`
`
        if len(samples[kind]) < max_samples:`
            samples[kind].append({`
                "linea": line_no,`
                "ciclos_detectados": hits,`
                "registro": obj`
            })`
`
with (base / "muestras_dataflow_tw.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as out:`
    out.write("CICLOS OBJETIVO: " + str(len(targets)) + "\n")`
    out.write("CICLOS ENCONTRADOS: " + str(len(matched_epochs)) + "\n")`
    out.write("CICLOS SIN COINCIDENCIA:\n")`
    for e in sorted(targets - matched_epochs):`
        out.write(e + "\n")`
`
    out.write("\n========== CONTEOS ==========\n")`
    for k, v in counts.most_common():`
        out.write(f"{k}: {v}\n")`
`
    out.write("\n========== MUESTRAS POR TIPO ==========\n")`
    for kind, records in samples.items():`
        out.write(f"\n\n########## TIPO: {kind} ##########\n")`
        for item in records:`
            out.write(f"\nLINEA: {item['linea']}\n")`
            out.write(f"CICLOS: {item['ciclos_detectados']}\n")`
            out.write(json.dumps(`
                item["registro"],`
                ensure_ascii=False,`
                indent=2`
            )[:10000])`
            out.write("\n")`
`
print("Extracción terminada.")`
print("Ciclos encontrados:", len(matched_epochs), "de", len(targets))`
print("Muestras guardadas:", txt)`

import json
import re
from collections import Counter
with open("result.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    data = json.load(f)
def plano(v):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        return v
    if isinstance(v, list):
        return "".join(`
            x if isinstance(x, str)
            else x.get("text", "") if isinstance(x, dict)
            else ""
            for x in v
        )
    return ""
for estrategia in ("CB", "TW", "FLOW"):
    print(f"\n{'='*25} {estrategia} {'='*25}")
    resultados = []
    for m in data["messages"]:
        t = plano(m.get("text", ""))
        if re.search(rf"\b{estrategia}\b", t, re.I):
            if re.search(r"\b(WON|WIN|LOST|LOSS|GANADA|GANADO|PERDIDA|PERDIDO)\b|🏆|💀", t, re.I):
                resultados.append((m.get("date", ""), t))
    print("Mensajes de resultado:", len(resultados))
    for fecha, t in resultados[:12]:
        print(f"\n{fecha}\n{t[:700]}")

        try {`
            $j = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json`

                [PSCustomObject]@{`
                    ts          = $j.ts`
                    epoch       = $j.epoch`
                    kind        = $j.kind`
                    slug        = $j.slug`
                    side        = $j.side`
                    winner      = $j.winner`
                    won         = $j.won`
                    ok          = $j.ok`
                    ask         = $j.ask`
                    price_cap   = $j.price_cap`
                    fill_price  = $j.fill_price`
                    shares      = $j.shares`
                    paid_usdc   = $j.paid_usdc`
                    usdc        = $j.usdc`
                    pnl_est     = $j.pnl_est`
                    sigma       = $j.sigma`
                    z           = $j.z`
                    elapsed     = $j.elapsed`

$allLines = Get-Content $botFile
$orderRecords = @()
for ($i = 0; $i -lt $allLines.Count; $i++) {`
`
    if ($allLines[$i] -match 'TW order') {`
`
        $start = $i`
        $end = [Math]::Min($i + 5, $allLines.Count - 1)`
`
        $block = ($allLines[$start..$end] -join " ")`
`
        $orderID = ""`
        $shares = $null`
        $cost = $null`
        $status = ""`
        $success = ""`
        $txHash = ""`
`
        if ($block -match "'orderID':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $orderID = $Matches[1]`
        }`
`
        if ($block -match "'takingAmount':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $shares = [double]::Parse(`
                $Matches[1],`
                [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
            )`
        }`
`
        if ($block -match "'makingAmount':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $cost = [double]::Parse(`
                $Matches[1],`
                [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
            )`
        }`
`
        if ($block -match "'status':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $status = $Matches[1]`
        }`
`
        if ($block -match "'success':\s*(True|False)") {`
            $success = $Matches[1]`
        }`
`
        if ($block -match "'transactionsHashes':\s*\['([^']+)'") {`
            $txHash = $Matches[1]`
        }`
`
        $classification = if (`
            $status -eq "matched" -and $success -eq "True"`
        ) {`
            "MATCHED_CONFIRMADA"`
        } elseif ($orderID -ne "") {`
            "RESPUESTA_NO_CONFIRMADA"`
        } else {`
            "INCOMPLETA"`
        }`
`
        $orderRecords += [PSCustomObject]@{`
            log_line       = $start + 1`
            orderID        = $orderID`
            status         = $status`
            success        = $success`
            shares         = $shares`
            cost_usdc      = $cost`
            avg_price      = if ($shares -gt 0) {`
                [Math]::Round($cost / $shares, 8)`
            } else { $null }`
            transactionHash = $txHash`
            classification = $classification`
            raw_response   = $block`
        }`
    }`
}
