# ============================================================
# DIAGNOSTICO DE INTEGRIDAD - AUDITORIA TW
# No modifica archivos originales ni configuracion del bot.
# ============================================================
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$outDir = ".\auditoria_tw_ejecuciones"
$events = Import-Csv "$outDir\01_eventos_tw.csv"
$orders = Import-Csv "$outDir\02_ordenes_tw.csv"
$joined = Import-Csv "$outDir\04_senales_resultados_tw.csv"
# Leer todos los eventos originales para descubrir nombres de SKIP
$allEvents = @(`
    Get-Content ".\tw_log.jsonl" | ForEach-Object {`
        try { $_ | ConvertFrom-Json } catch {}`
    }`
)
$kindCounts = $allEvents |`
    Group-Object kind |`
    Sort-Object Count -Descending
$signals = @($events | Where-Object kind -eq "TW_SIGNAL")
$results = @($events | Where-Object kind -eq "TW_RESULT")
$signalsWithoutResult = @(`
    $signals | Where-Object {`
        $slug = $_.slug`
        @($results | Where-Object slug -eq $slug).Count -eq 0`
    }`
)
$resultsWithoutSignal = @(`
    $results | Where-Object {`
        $slug = $_.slug`
        @($signals | Where-Object slug -eq $slug).Count -eq 0`
    }`
)
$duplicateSignalSlugs = @(`
    $signals | Group-Object slug | Where-Object Count -gt 1`
)
$duplicateResultSlugs = @(`
    $results | Group-Object slug | Where-Object Count -gt 1`
)
$duplicateOrderIDs = @(`
    $orders |`
        Where-Object { $_.orderID -ne "" } |`
        Group-Object orderID |`
        Where-Object Count -gt 1`
)
$emptyOrderIDs = @($orders | Where-Object { !$_.orderID })
$nonMatched = @(`
    $orders | Where-Object classification -ne "MATCHED_CONFIRMADA"`
)
$confirmed = @(`
    $orders | Where-Object classification -eq "MATCHED_CONFIRMADA"`
)
$totalShares = (`
    $confirmed | Measure-Object { [double]$_.shares } -Sum`
).Sum
$totalCost = (`
    $confirmed | Measure-Object { [double]$_.cost_usdc } -Sum`
).Sum
$avgPrice = if ($totalShares -gt 0) {`
    $totalCost / $totalShares`
} else { 0 }
$signalOk = @($signals | Where-Object ok -eq "True").Count
$signalFailed = @($signals | Where-Object ok -eq "False").Count
$skipKinds = @(`
    $kindCounts | Where-Object Name -match "SKIP"`
)
$report = @()
$report += "DIAGNOSTICO DE INTEGRIDAD TW"
$report += "============================"
$report += ""
$report += "EVENTOS TW"
$report += "TW_SIGNAL: $($signals.Count)"
$report += "TW_RESULT: $($results.Count)"
$report += "TW_SIGNAL ok=True: $signalOk"
$report += "TW_SIGNAL ok=False: $signalFailed"
$report += ""
$report += "SLUGS"
$report += "Señales sin resultado: $($signalsWithoutResult.Count)"
$report += "Resultados sin señal: $($resultsWithoutSignal.Count)"
$report += "Slugs de señal duplicados: $($duplicateSignalSlugs.Count)"
$report += "Slugs de resultado duplicados: $($duplicateResultSlugs.Count)"
$report += ""
$report += "ORDENES EXTRAIDAS"
$report += "Respuestas encontradas: $($orders.Count)"
$report += "Matched clasificadas: $($confirmed.Count)"
$report += "No confirmadas: $($nonMatched.Count)"
$report += "Sin orderID: $($emptyOrderIDs.Count)"
$report += "orderID duplicados: $($duplicateOrderIDs.Count)"
$report += "Shares reportadas: $totalShares"
$report += "Costo reportado USDC: $totalCost"
$report += "Precio promedio ponderado: $avgPrice"
$report += ""
$report += "EVENTOS CUYO NOMBRE CONTIENE SKIP"
foreach ($k in $skipKinds) {`
    $report += "$($k.Name): $($k.Count)"`
}
$report += ""
$report += "TODOS LOS TIPOS DE EVENTO"
foreach ($k in $kindCounts) {`
    $report += "$($k.Name): $($k.Count)"`
}
$report | Out-File "$outDir\05_diagnostico_integridad.txt" -Encoding UTF8
# Exportar duplicados y excepciones para inspección
$duplicateOrderIDs |`
    Select-Object Name, Count |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\06_orderids_duplicados.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$signalsWithoutResult |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\07_senales_sin_resultado.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$resultsWithoutSignal |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\08_resultados_sin_senal.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$skipKinds |`
    Select-Object Name, Count |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\09_tipos_skip.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`n========== DIAGNOSTICO ==========" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-Content "$outDir\05_diagnostico_integridad.txt"
Write-Host "`nArchivos adicionales generados en $outDir" -ForegroundColor Cyan
# ============================================================
# LIBRO FINAL DE CONCILIACION TW
# Deduplicacion por slug + cruce CLOB + TW_RESULT
# Solo lectura de originales. Genera archivos nuevos.
# ============================================================
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$outDir = ".\auditoria_tw_ejecuciones"
$events = Import-Csv "$outDir\01_eventos_tw.csv"
$orders = Import-Csv "$outDir\02_ordenes_tw.csv"
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Señales aceptadas: una por slug.
# Si hubo reintento, conserva ok=True.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$allSignals = @($events | Where-Object kind -eq "TW_SIGNAL")
$allResults = @($events | Where-Object kind -eq "TW_RESULT")
$acceptedSignals = @(`
    foreach ($g in ($allSignals | Group-Object slug)) {`
        $s = $g.Group |`
            Where-Object ok -eq "True" |`
            Select-Object -First 1`
`
        if ($s) { $s }`
    }`
)
# Resultados: uno por slug.
$resultMap = @{}
foreach ($g in ($allResults | Group-Object slug)) {`
    $resultMap[$g.Name] = $g.Group | Select-Object -First 1`
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Recuperar el slug del contexto de cada respuesta CLOB.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$ctxLines = Get-Content "$outDir\03_contexto_ordenes_tw.txt"
$orderByLine = @{}
$contexts = @()
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Vincular órdenes por slug.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$orderMap = @{}
$contextWithoutSlug = @()
foreach ($c in $contexts) {`
`
    if ($c.slug) {`
        $orderMap[$c.slug] = [PSCustomObject]@{`
            order = $c.order`
            match = "DIRECTO"`
        }`
    }`
    else {`
        $contextWithoutSlug += $c`
    }`
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Inferencia controlada del contexto sin slug.
# Solo acepta una coincidencia única de:
# lado + participaciones + costo.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Construir libro: una fila por operación aceptada.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$ledger |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\10_libro_conciliado.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Comparacion de cantidades CLOB vs TW_SIGNAL.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$comparison = @(`
    foreach ($x in $ledger | Where-Object orderID) {`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            slug = $x.slug`
            order_match = $x.order_match`
            shares_signal = $x.shares_signal`
            shares_clob = $x.shares_clob`
            delta_shares = [Math]::Round(`
                $x.shares_clob - $x.shares_signal, 8`
            )`
            cost_clob = $x.cost_clob`
            cost_signal_final = $x.cost_final`
            delta_cost = [Math]::Round(`
                $x.cost_clob - $x.cost_final, 8`
            )`
            orderID = $x.orderID`
        }`
    }`
)
$comparison |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\11_comparacion_cantidades.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Separar intentos fallidos.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$allSignals |`
    Where-Object ok -eq "False" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\12_intentos_fallidos.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Resumen corregido.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$direct = @(`
    $ledger | Where-Object order_match -eq "DIRECTO"`
).Count
$inferred = @(`
    $ledger | Where-Object order_match -like "INFERIDO*"`
).Count
$withoutClob = @(`
    $ledger | Where-Object order_match -eq "SIN_RESPUESTA_CLOB"`
).Count
$wins = @($ledger | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count
$losses = @($ledger | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count
$totalShares = (`
    $ledger | Measure-Object shares_final -Sum`
).Sum
$totalCost = (`
    $ledger | Measure-Object cost_final -Sum`
).Sum
$totalPnl = (`
    $ledger | Measure-Object pnl_est -Sum`
).Sum
$clobRows = @($ledger | Where-Object orderID)
$clobShares = (`
    $clobRows | Measure-Object shares_clob -Sum`
).Sum
$clobCost = (`
    $clobRows | Measure-Object cost_clob -Sum`
).Sum
$avg = if ($totalShares -gt 0) {`
    $totalCost / $totalShares`
} else { 0 }
$winRate = if ($ledger.Count -gt 0) {`
    100 * $wins / $ledger.Count`
} else { 0 }
@"`
LIBRO FINAL DE CONCILIACION TW`
==============================`
`
Operaciones aceptadas unicas: $($ledger.Count)`
Resultados ganadores: $wins`
Resultados perdedores: $losses`
Win rate: $([Math]::Round($winRate, 2))%`
`
ORDENES CLOB`
Coincidencias directas: $direct`
Coincidencias inferidas: $inferred`
Sin respuesta CLOB localizada: $withoutClob`
`
CANTIDADES`
Participaciones finales: $totalShares`
Costo final registrado: $totalCost`
Precio promedio ponderado: $avg`
`
RESPUESTAS CLOB`
Participaciones: $clobShares`
Costo: $clobCost`
`
RESULTADO`
Suma TW_RESULT.pnl_est: $totalPnl`
`
IMPORTANTE:`
El costo de operaciones sin respuesta CLOB se toma de TW_SIGNAL.`
El PnL es estimado por el bot y no descuenta comisiones no registradas.`
La coincidencia inferida no es una vinculacion directa por slug.`
"@ | Out-File "$outDir\13_resumen_libro_final.txt" -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`nLIBRO FINAL GENERADO" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-Content "$outDir\13_resumen_libro_final.txt"
# ============================================================
# AUDITORIA DE RENTABILIDAD TW
# Analiza PnL estimado por precio, tamaño, lado y sigma.
# No modifica originales ni configuracion del bot.
# ============================================================
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$outDir = ".\auditoria_tw_ejecuciones"
$ledger = Import-Csv "$outDir\10_libro_conciliado.csv"
$culture = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture
function Num($v) {`
    if ($null -eq $v -or "$v".Trim() -eq "") {`
        return $null`
    }`
    try {`
        return [double]::Parse("$v", $culture)`
    } catch {`
        return $null`
    }`
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Preparar una fila por mercado con resultado.
# Precio: primero CLOB; si no existe, fill_price o ask.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$data = @(`
    foreach ($x in $ledger) {`
`
        if ($x.won -notin @("True","False")) {`
            continue`
        }`
`
        $price = Num $x.avg_price_clob`
`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.fill_price_signal`
        }`
`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.ask`
        }`
`
        $cost = Num $x.cost_final`
        $pnl = Num $x.pnl_est`
        $shares = Num $x.shares_final`
        $sigma = Num $x.sigma`
        $z = Num $x.z`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            slug = $x.slug`
            side = $x.side`
            won = $x.won`
            price = $price`
            cost = $cost`
            pnl = $pnl`
            shares = $shares`
            sigma = $sigma`
            z = $z`
            order_match = $x.order_match`
        }`
    }`
)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Funcion para resumir grupos.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
function Group-Stats {`
    param(`
        [array]$Rows,`
        [string]$GroupName`
    )`
`
    foreach ($g in ($Rows | Group-Object $GroupName | Sort-Object Name)) {`
`
        $items = @($g.Group)`
        $wins = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count`
        $losses = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count`
`
        $pnl = ($items | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum`
        $cost = ($items | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum`
        $shares = ($items | Measure-Object shares -Sum).Sum`
`
        $roi = if ($cost -gt 0) {`
            100 * $pnl / $cost`
        } else { $null }`
`
        $winRate = if ($items.Count -gt 0) {`
            100 * $wins / $items.Count`
        } else { 0 }`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            grupo = $g.Name`
            operaciones = $items.Count`
            ganadoras = $wins`
            perdedoras = $losses`
            win_rate_pct = [Math]::Round($winRate, 2)`
            costo_usdc = [Math]::Round($cost, 6)`
            shares = [Math]::Round($shares, 6)`
            pnl_estimado = [Math]::Round($pnl, 6)`
            roi_estimado_pct = if ($null -ne $roi) {`
                [Math]::Round($roi, 4)`
            } else { $null }`
            pnl_promedio = [Math]::Round(`
                $pnl / [Math]::Max(1,$items.Count), 6`
            )`
        }`
    }`
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Clasificar precio.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
foreach ($x in $data) {`
`
    $p = $x.price`
`
    $x | Add-Member -NotePropertyName price_band -NotePropertyValue (`
        if ($null -eq $p) {`
            "SIN_PRECIO"`
        }`
        elseif ($p -lt 0.93) {`
            "MENOR_0.93"`
        }`
        elseif ($p -lt 0.95) {`
            "0.93_A_0.949"`
        }`
        elseif ($p -lt 0.97) {`
            "0.95_A_0.969"`
        }`
        elseif ($p -lt 0.98) {`
            "0.97_A_0.979"`
        }`
        else {`
            "0.98_O_MAYOR"`
        }`
    )`

    $c = $x.cost
    $x | Add-Member -NotePropertyName size_band -NotePropertyValue (`
        if ($null -eq $c) {`
            "SIN_COSTO"`
        }`
        elseif ($c -lt 5) {`
            "MENOS_5"`
        }
        elseif ($c -lt 10) {`
            "5_A_9.99"`
        }
        elseif ($c -lt 20) {`
            "10_A_19.99"`
        }
        elseif ($c -lt 40) {`
            "20_A_39.99"`
        }
        else {`
            "40_O_MAS"`
        }
    )
    $s = $x.sigma
    $x | Add-Member -NotePropertyName sigma_band -NotePropertyValue (`
        if ($null -eq $s) {`
            "SIN_SIGMA"`
        }`
        elseif ($s -lt 2) {`
            "MENOR_2"`
        }
        elseif ($s -lt 3) {`
            "2_A_2.99"`
        }
        elseif ($s -lt 4) {`
            "3_A_3.99"`
        }
        elseif ($s -lt 5) {`
            "4_A_4.99"`
        }
        else {`
            "5_O_MAS"`
        }
    )
}
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Exportar tablas.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
Group-Stats $data "price_band" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\14_rentabilidad_por_precio.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Group-Stats $data "size_band" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\15_rentabilidad_por_tamano.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Group-Stats $data "side" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\16_rentabilidad_por_direccion.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Group-Stats $data "sigma_band" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\17_rentabilidad_por_sigma.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$data |`
    Where-Object won -eq "False" |`
    Sort-Object pnl |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\18_operaciones_perdedoras.csv" ``
        -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Resumen general.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
$total = $data.Count
$wins = @($data | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count
$losses = @($data | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count
$totalPnl = ($data | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum
$totalCost = ($data | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum
$roi = if ($totalCost -gt 0) {`
    100 * $totalPnl / $totalCost`
} else { 0 }
$missingPrice = @($data | Where-Object price -eq $null).Count
$missingCost = @($data | Where-Object cost -eq $null).Count
$missingPnl = @($data | Where-Object pnl -eq $null).Count
@"`
AUDITORIA DE RENTABILIDAD TW`
============================`
`
Operaciones con resultado: $total`
Ganadoras: $wins`
Perdedoras: $losses`
`
Win rate: $([Math]::Round(100*$wins/[Math]::Max(1,$total),2))%`
`
Costo analizado: $([Math]::Round($totalCost,6)) USDC`
PnL estimado: $([Math]::Round($totalPnl,6)) USDC`
ROI estimado: $([Math]::Round($roi,4))%`
`
DATOS FALTANTES`
Precio: $missingPrice`
Costo: $missingCost`
PnL: $missingPnl`
`
IMPORTANTE:`
El ROI usa pnl_est y costo_final del libro.`
No equivale a ROI neto confirmado.`
El precio prioriza avg_price_clob.`
Para operaciones sin CLOB utiliza fill_price_signal y luego ask.`
"@ | Out-File "$outDir\19_analisis_rentabilidad.txt" -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`nANALISIS DE RENTABILIDAD GENERADO" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-Content "$outDir\19_analisis_rentabilidad.txt"
Write-Host "`n--- POR PRECIO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\14_rentabilidad_por_precio.csv" |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n--- POR TAMANO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\15_rentabilidad_por_tamano.csv" |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n--- POR DIRECCION ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\16_rentabilidad_por_direccion.csv" |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n--- POR SIGMA ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\17_rentabilidad_por_sigma.csv" |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize
# ============================================================
# AUDITORIA DE RENTABILIDAD TW - CORREGIDA
# ============================================================
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$outDir = ".\auditoria_tw_ejecuciones"
$ledger = Import-Csv "$outDir\10_libro_conciliado.csv"
$culture = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture
function Num($v) {`
    if ($null -eq $v -or "$v".Trim() -eq "") {`
        return $null`
    }`
    try {`
        return [double]::Parse("$v", $culture)`
    } catch {`
        return $null`
    }`
}
# Clasificadores independientes: evita el error de sintaxis anterior.
function Get-PriceBand($p) {`
    if ($null -eq $p) { return "SIN_PRECIO" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.93) { return "MENOR_0.93" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.95) { return "0.93_A_0.949" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.97) { return "0.95_A_0.969" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.98) { return "0.97_A_0.979" }`
    return "0.98_O_MAYOR"`
}
function Get-SizeBand($c) {`
    if ($null -eq $c) { return "SIN_COSTO" }`
    if ($c -lt 5) { return "MENOS_5" }`
    if ($c -lt 10) { return "5_A_9.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 20) { return "10_A_19.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 40) { return "20_A_39.99" }`
    return "40_O_MAS"`
}
function Get-SigmaBand($s) {`
    if ($null -eq $s) { return "SIN_SIGMA" }`
    if ($s -lt 2) { return "MENOR_2" }`
    if ($s -lt 3) { return "2_A_2.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 4) { return "3_A_3.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 5) { return "4_A_4.99" }`
    return "5_O_MAS"`
}
# Preparar operaciones con resultado.
$data = @(`
    foreach ($x in $ledger) {`
        if ($x.won -notin @("True","False")) {`
            continue`
        }`
`
        $price = Num $x.avg_price_clob`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.fill_price_signal`
        }`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.ask`
        }`
`
        $cost = Num $x.cost_final`
        $pnl = Num $x.pnl_est`
        $shares = Num $x.shares_final`
        $sigma = Num $x.sigma`
        $z = Num $x.z`
`
        $obj = [PSCustomObject]@{`
            slug = $x.slug`
            side = $x.side`
            won = $x.won`
            price = $price`
            cost = $cost`
            pnl = $pnl`
            shares = $shares`
            sigma = $sigma`
            z = $z`
            order_match = $x.order_match`
        }`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName price_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-PriceBand $price)`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName size_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-SizeBand $cost)`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName sigma_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-SigmaBand $sigma)`
`
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace("$obj.side")) {`
            $obj.side = "SIN_LADO"`
        }`
`
        $obj`
    }`
)
# Resumen por grupo.
function Group-Stats {`
    param(`
        [array]$Rows,`
        [string]$GroupName`
    )`
`
    foreach ($g in ($Rows | Group-Object -Property $GroupName | Sort-Object Name)) {`
        $items = @($g.Group)`
`
        $wins = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count`
        $losses = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count`
`
        $pnl = ($items | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum`
        $cost = ($items | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum`
        $shares = ($items | Measure-Object shares -Sum).Sum`
`
        if ($null -eq $pnl) { $pnl = 0 }`
        if ($null -eq $cost) { $cost = 0 }`
        if ($null -eq $shares) { $shares = 0 }`
`
        $roi = if ($cost -gt 0) {`
            100 * $pnl / $cost`
        } else { $null }`
`
        $winRate = 100 * $wins / [Math]::Max(1,$items.Count)`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            grupo = $g.Name`
            operaciones = $items.Count`
            ganadoras = $wins`
            perdedoras = $losses`
            win_rate_pct = [Math]::Round($winRate,2)`
            costo_usdc = [Math]::Round($cost,6)`
            shares = [Math]::Round($shares,6)`
            pnl_estimado = [Math]::Round($pnl,6)`
            roi_estimado_pct = if ($null -ne $roi) {`
                [Math]::Round($roi,4)`
            } else { $null }`
            pnl_promedio = [Math]::Round(`
                $pnl / [Math]::Max(1,$items.Count),6`
            )`
        }`
    }`
}

    Export-Csv "$outDir\17_rentabilidad_por_sigma.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$data |`
    Where-Object won -eq "False" |`
    Sort-Object pnl |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\18_operaciones_perdedoras.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# Validación y resumen.

$losses = @($data | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count
$totalPnl = ($data | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum
$totalCost = ($data | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum
$missingPrice = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.price }).Count
$missingCost = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.cost }).Count
$missingPnl = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.pnl }).Count
$roi = if ($totalCost -gt 0) {`
    100 * $totalPnl / $totalCost`
} else { 0 }
@"`
AUDITORIA DE RENTABILIDAD TW`
============================`
`
Operaciones: $total`
Ganadoras: $wins`
Perdedoras: $losses`
Win rate: $([Math]::Round(100*$wins/[Math]::Max(1,$total),2))%`
`
Costo: $([Math]::Round($totalCost,6)) USDC`
PnL estimado: $([Math]::Round($totalPnl,6)) USDC`
ROI estimado: $([Math]::Round($roi,4))%`
`
DATOS FALTANTES`
Precio: $missingPrice`
Costo: $missingCost`
PnL: $missingPnl`
`
CLASIFICACIONES`
Precio:`
$((($data | Group-Object price_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
Tamaño:`
$((($data | Group-Object size_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
Sigma:`
$((($data | Group-Object sigma_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
NOTA:`
PnL y ROI son estimaciones basadas en pnl_est.`
No representan todavía rentabilidad neta confirmada.`
"@ | Out-File "$outDir\19_analisis_rentabilidad.txt" -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`nAUDITORIA CORREGIDA GENERADA" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "`n--- POR PRECIO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\14_rentabilidad_por_precio.csv" | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n--- POR TAMANO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Import-Csv "$outDir\15_rentabilidad_por_tamano.csv" | Format-Table -AutoSize

    if ($p -lt 0.95) { return "0.93_A_0.949" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.97) { return "0.95_A_0.969" }`

function Get-SizeBand($c) {`
    if ($null -eq $c) { return "SIN_COSTO" }`
    if ($c -lt 5) { return "MENOS_5" }`
    if ($c -lt 10) { return "5_A_9.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 20) { return "10_A_19.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 40) { return "20_A_39.99" }`
    return "40_O_MAS"`
}
function Get-SigmaBand($s) {`
    if ($null -eq $s) { return "SIN_SIGMA" }`
    if ($s -lt 2) { return "MENOR_2" }`
    if ($s -lt 3) { return "2_A_2.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 4) { return "3_A_3.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 5) { return "4_A_4.99" }`
    return "5_O_MAS"`
}
# Preparar operaciones con resultado.
$data = @(`
    foreach ($x in $ledger) {`
        if ($x.won -notin @("True","False")) {`
            continue`
        }`
`
        $price = Num $x.avg_price_clob`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.fill_price_signal`
        }`
        if ($null -eq $price) {`
            $price = Num $x.ask`
        }`
`
        $cost = Num $x.cost_final`
        $pnl = Num $x.pnl_est`
        $shares = Num $x.shares_final`
        $sigma = Num $x.sigma`
        $z = Num $x.z`
`
        $obj = [PSCustomObject]@{`
            slug = $x.slug`
            side = $x.side`
            won = $x.won`
            price = $price`
            cost = $cost`
            pnl = $pnl`
            shares = $shares`
            sigma = $sigma`
            z = $z`
            order_match = $x.order_match`
        }`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName price_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-PriceBand $price)`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName size_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-SizeBand $cost)`
`
        $obj | Add-Member -NotePropertyName sigma_band ``
            -NotePropertyValue (Get-SigmaBand $sigma)`
`
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace("$obj.side")) {`
            $obj.side = "SIN_LADO"`
        }`
`
        $obj`
    }`
)
# Resumen por grupo.
function Group-Stats {`
    param(`
        [array]$Rows,`
        [string]$GroupName`
    )`
`
    foreach ($g in ($Rows | Group-Object -Property $GroupName | Sort-Object Name)) {`
        $items = @($g.Group)`
`
        $wins = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count`
        $losses = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count`
`
        $pnl = ($items | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum`
        $cost = ($items | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum`
        $shares = ($items | Measure-Object shares -Sum).Sum`
`
        if ($null -eq $pnl) { $pnl = 0 }`
        if ($null -eq $cost) { $cost = 0 }`
        if ($null -eq $shares) { $shares = 0 }`
`
        $roi = if ($cost -gt 0) {`
            100 * $pnl / $cost`
        } else { $null }`
`
        $winRate = 100 * $wins / [Math]::Max(1,$items.Count)`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            grupo = $g.Name`
            operaciones = $items.Count`
            ganadoras = $wins`
            perdedoras = $losses`
            win_rate_pct = [Math]::Round($winRate,2)`
            costo_usdc = [Math]::Round($cost,6)`
            shares = [Math]::Round($shares,6)`
            pnl_estimado = [Math]::Round($pnl,6)`
            roi_estimado_pct = if ($null -ne $roi) {`
                [Math]::Round($roi,4)`
            } else { $null }`
            pnl_promedio = [Math]::Round(`
                $pnl / [Math]::Max(1,$items.Count),6`
            )`
        }`
    }`
}
# Exportar análisis.
Group-Stats $data "price_band" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\14_rentabilidad_por_precio.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Group-Stats $data "size_band" |`

Group-Stats $data "side" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\16_rentabilidad_por_direccion.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Group-Stats $data "sigma_band" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\17_rentabilidad_por_sigma.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$data |`
    Where-Object won -eq "False" |`
    Sort-Object pnl |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\18_operaciones_perdedoras.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# Validación y resumen.
$total = $data.Count
$wins = @($data | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count
$losses = @($data | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count
$totalPnl = ($data | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum
$totalCost = ($data | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum
$missingPrice = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.price }).Count
$missingCost = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.cost }).Count
$missingPnl = @($data | Where-Object { $null -eq $_.pnl }).Count
$roi = if ($totalCost -gt 0) {`
    100 * $totalPnl / $totalCost`
} else { 0 }
@"`
AUDITORIA DE RENTABILIDAD TW`
============================`
`
Operaciones: $total`
Ganadoras: $wins`
Perdedoras: $losses`
Win rate: $([Math]::Round(100*$wins/[Math]::Max(1,$total),2))%`
`
Costo: $([Math]::Round($totalCost,6)) USDC`
PnL estimado: $([Math]::Round($totalPnl,6)) USDC`
ROI estimado: $([Math]::Round($roi,4))%`
`
DATOS FALTANTES`
Precio: $missingPrice`
Costo: $missingCost`
PnL: $missingPnl`
`
CLASIFICACIONES`
Precio:`
$((($data | Group-Object price_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
Tamaño:`
$((($data | Group-Object size_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
Sigma:`
$((($data | Group-Object sigma_band | ForEach-Object { "$($_.Name): $($_.Count)" }) -join "`n"))`
`
NOTA:`
PnL y ROI son estimaciones basadas en pnl_est.`
No representan todavía rentabilidad neta confirmada.`
"@ | Out-File "$outDir\19_analisis_rentabilidad.txt" -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`nAUDITORIA CORREGIDA GENERADA" -ForegroundColor Green

Import-Csv "$outDir\14_rentabilidad_por_precio.csv" | Format-Table -AutoSize

# AUDITORIA TW - CRUCES DE RENTABILIDAD

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$outDir = ".\auditoria_tw_ejecuciones"
$culture = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture
function N($v) {`
    if ($null -eq $v -or "$v".Trim() -eq "") { return $null }`
    try { return [double]::Parse("$v", $culture) }`
    catch { return $null }`
}
function PriceBand($p) {`
    if ($null -eq $p) { return "SIN_PRECIO" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.93) { return "MENOR_0.93" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.95) { return "0.93_A_0.949" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.97) { return "0.95_A_0.969" }`
    if ($p -lt 0.98) { return "0.97_A_0.979" }`
    return "0.98_O_MAYOR"`
}
function SizeBand($c) {`
    if ($null -eq $c) { return "SIN_COSTO" }`
    if ($c -lt 5) { return "MENOS_5" }`
    if ($c -lt 10) { return "5_A_9.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 20) { return "10_A_19.99" }`
    if ($c -lt 40) { return "20_A_39.99" }`
    return "40_O_MAS"`
}
function SigmaBand($s) {`
    if ($null -eq $s) { return "SIN_SIGMA" }`
    if ($s -lt 2) { return "MENOR_2" }`
    if ($s -lt 3) { return "2_A_2.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 4) { return "3_A_3.99" }`
    if ($s -lt 5) { return "4_A_4.99" }`
    return "5_O_MAS"`
}
$ledger = Import-Csv "$outDir\10_libro_conciliado.csv"
$data = @(`
    foreach ($x in $ledger) {`
        if ($x.won -notin @("True","False")) { continue }`
`
        $p = N $x.avg_price_clob`
        if ($null -eq $p) { $p = N $x.fill_price_signal }`
        if ($null -eq $p) { $p = N $x.ask }`
`
        $c = N $x.cost_final`
        $s = N $x.sigma`
        $pnl = N $x.pnl_est`
`
        $side = "$($x.side)"`
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($side)) { $side = "SIN_LADO" }`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            slug = $x.slug`
            side = $side`
            won = $x.won`
            price = $p`
            cost = $c`
            pnl = $pnl`
            sigma = $s`
            price_band = (PriceBand $p)`
            size_band = (SizeBand $c)`
            sigma_band = (SigmaBand $s)`
            order_match = $x.order_match`
        }`
    }`
)
# Crear dimensiones combinadas.
foreach ($x in $data) {`
    $x | Add-Member price_size "$($x.price_band) | $($x.size_band)"`
    $x | Add-Member side_size "$($x.side) | $($x.size_band)"`
    $x | Add-Member side_price "$($x.side) | $($x.price_band)"`
    $x | Add-Member sigma_size "$($x.sigma_band) | $($x.size_band)"`
}
function Cross-Stats {`
    param(`
        [array]$Rows,`
        [string]$Dimension`
    )`
`
    foreach ($g in ($Rows | Group-Object -Property $Dimension | Sort-Object Name)) {`
        $items = @($g.Group)`
        $wins = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "True").Count`
        $losses = @($items | Where-Object won -eq "False").Count`
`
        $pnl = ($items | Measure-Object pnl -Sum).Sum`
        $cost = ($items | Measure-Object cost -Sum).Sum`
`
        if ($null -eq $pnl) { $pnl = 0 }`
        if ($null -eq $cost) { $cost = 0 }`
`
        $roi = if ($cost -gt 0) { 100 * $pnl / $cost } else { $null }`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            grupo = $g.Name`
            operaciones = $items.Count`
            ganadoras = $wins`
            perdedoras = $losses`
            win_rate_pct = [Math]::Round(100*$wins/[Math]::Max(1,$items.Count),2)`
            costo_usdc = [Math]::Round($cost,6)`
            pnl_estimado = [Math]::Round($pnl,6)`
            roi_estimado_pct = if ($null -ne $roi) {`
                [Math]::Round($roi,4)`
            } else { $null }`
        }`
    }`
}
Cross-Stats $data "price_size" |`
    Export-Csv "$outDir\20_cruce_precio_tamano.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
Cross-Stats $data "side_size" |`

$out = ".\diagnostico_balance_tw.txt"

$log = ".\bot_output.log"
# 1. Extraer lecturas reales de balance
$balanceRows = Select-String -Path $log -Pattern '💰 balance=' |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        if ($_.Line -match 'balance=([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*USDC') {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                linea   = $_.LineNumber`
                balance = [decimal]$Matches[1]`
            }`
        }`
    }
# 2. Conservar cambios consecutivos de balance
$balanceChanges = @()
$previous = $null
foreach ($r in $balanceRows) {`
    if ($null -eq $previous -or $r.balance -ne $previous) {`
        $balanceChanges += [PSCustomObject]@{`
            linea   = $r.linea`
            balance = $r.balance`
            cambio  = if ($null -eq $previous) {`
                $null`
            } else {`
                [math]::Round([double]($r.balance - $previous), 6)`
            }`
        }`
        $previous = $r.balance`
    }`
}
$balanceChanges |`
    Export-Csv ".\cronologia_balance.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# 3. Extraer exclusivamente respuestas de órdenes TW
$twOrders = Select-String -Path $log -Pattern 'TW order \(' |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $shares = $null`
        $cost = $null`
        $orderId = $null`
`
        if ($_.Line -match "'takingAmount':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $shares = $Matches[1]`
        }`
        if ($_.Line -match "'makingAmount':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $cost = $Matches[1]`
        }`
        if ($_.Line -match "'orderID':\s*'([^']+)'") {`
            $orderId = $Matches[1]`
        }`
`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            linea    = $_.LineNumber`
            shares   = $shares`
            cost_usdc = $cost`
            order_id = $orderId`
            registro = $_.Line`
        }`
    }
$twOrders |`
    Export-Csv ".\ordenes_tw_log.csv" -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
# 4. Conteos de señales y resultados
$signalCount = (Select-String -Path $log -Pattern 'TW_SIGNAL').Count
$resultCount = (Select-String -Path $log -Pattern 'TW_RESULT').Count
Write-Host "`n--- BALANCE ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"Lecturas de balance: $($balanceRows.Count)"
"Cambios consecutivos: $($balanceChanges.Count)"
$balanceChanges | Select-Object -First 10 | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`nULTIMOS CAMBIOS:"
$balanceChanges | Select-Object -Last 15 | Format-Table -AutoSize

"Respuestas TW encontradas: $($twOrders.Count)"
$twOrders | Select-Object -First 5 | Format-Table linea,shares,cost_usdc -AutoSize
$twOrders | Select-Object -Last 5 | Format-Table linea,shares,cost_usdc -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n--- SEÑALES Y RESULTADOS ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"TW_SIGNAL: $signalCount"
"TW_RESULT: $resultCount"
Write-Host "`n--- ARCHIVOS GENERADOS ---" -ForegroundColor Green
Get-Item ".\cronologia_balance.csv", ".\ordenes_tw_log.csv" |`
    Select-Object Name,Length
$culture = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture
$balances = Import-Csv ".\cronologia_balance.csv"
$orders = Import-Csv ".\ordenes_tw_log.csv"
# Movimientos del balance
$positive = @($balances | Where-Object {`
    $_.cambio -ne "" -and [double]::Parse($_.cambio, $culture) -gt 0`
})
$negative = @($balances | Where-Object {`
    $_.cambio -ne "" -and [double]::Parse($_.cambio, $culture) -lt 0`
})
$totalIn = ($positive | ForEach-Object {`
    [double]::Parse($_.cambio, $culture)`
} | Measure-Object -Sum).Sum
$totalOut = ($negative | ForEach-Object {`
    [double]::Parse($_.cambio, $culture)`
} | Measure-Object -Sum).Sum
# Importe de las órdenes TW
$twCost = ($orders | ForEach-Object {`
    [double]::Parse($_.cost_usdc, $culture)`
} | Measure-Object -Sum).Sum
$twShares = ($orders | ForEach-Object {`
    [double]::Parse($_.shares, $culture)`
} | Measure-Object -Sum).Sum
$twPrices = @($orders | ForEach-Object {`
    [double]::Parse($_.cost_usdc, $culture) /`
    [double]::Parse($_.shares, $culture)`
})
Write-Host "`n--- MOVIMIENTOS DEL BALANCE ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"Aumentos: $($positive.Count)"
"Total aumentos: $([math]::Round($totalIn, 6)) USDC"
"Disminuciones: $($negative.Count)"
"Total disminuciones: $([math]::Round($totalOut, 6)) USDC"
"Neto de movimientos: $([math]::Round(($totalIn + $totalOut), 6)) USDC"
Write-Host "`n--- ORDENES TW ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"Órdenes: $($orders.Count)"
"Participaciones: $([math]::Round($twShares, 6))"
"Costo total: $([math]::Round($twCost, 6)) USDC"
"Precio promedio ponderado: $([math]::Round(($twCost / $twShares), 8))"
"Compra más pequeña: $([math]::Round((($orders | ForEach-Object {[double]::Parse($_.cost_usdc,$culture)}) | Measure-Object -Minimum).Minimum, 6)) USDC"
"Compra más grande: $([math]::Round((($orders | ForEach-Object {[double]::Parse($_.cost_usdc,$culture)}) | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum, 6)) USDC"
# Cambios de balance cercanos a órdenes TW: solo proximidad, no causalidad
$balanceWithChange = @($balances | Where-Object { $_.cambio -ne "" })
$near = foreach ($o in $orders) {`
    $line = [int]$o.linea`
    $candidate = $balanceWithChange |`
        ForEach-Object {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                distancia = [math]::Abs([int]$_.linea - $line)`
                linea_balance = [int]$_.linea`
                cambio = $_.cambio`
            }`
        } |`
        Where-Object { $_.distancia -le 100 } |`
        Sort-Object distancia |`
        Select-Object -First 1`
`
    if ($candidate) {`
        [PSCustomObject]@{`
            linea_orden = $line`
            costo_TW = $o.cost_usdc`
            linea_balance_cercana = $candidate.linea_balance`
            distancia_lineas = $candidate.distancia`
            cambio_balance = $candidate.cambio`
        }`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n--- PROXIMIDAD ORDEN / BALANCE ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"Órdenes con cambio de balance a menos de 100 líneas: $(@($near).Count)"
$near | Select-Object -First 15 | Format-Table -AutoSize
$log = ".\bot_output.log"
$out = ".\contexto_ordenes_tw_balance.txt"
Select-String -Path $log -Pattern 'TW order \(' -Context 10,10 |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        "========== ORDEN TW EN LINEA $($_.LineNumber) =========="`
        $_.Context.PreContext`
        $_.Line`
        $_.Context.PostContext`
        ""`
    } |`
    Set-Content -Path $out -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`n--- ARCHIVO GENERADO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-Item $out | Select-Object Name,Length
Write-Host "`n--- PRIMERAS 100 LINEAS ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-Content $out -TotalCount 100
Write-Host "`n--- CONTEXTO DE LAS COMPRAS MAS GRANDES ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$orders = Import-Csv ".\ordenes_tw_log.csv"
$largest = $orders |`
    Sort-Object { [double]$_.cost_usdc } -Descending |`
    Select-Object -First 5
foreach ($o in $largest) {`
    Write-Host "`nORDEN: linea $($o.linea) | costo $($o.cost_usdc) USDC" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
`
    Select-String -Path $log ``
        -Pattern 'TW order \(' ``
        -Context 10,10 |`
        Where-Object { $_.LineNumber -eq [int]$o.linea } |`
        ForEach-Object {`
            $_.Context.PreContext`
            $_.Line`
            $_.Context.PostContext`
        }`
}

Write-Host "Carpeta temporal: $ExportDir"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $ExportDir -Force | Out-Null
# ---------- 1. Buscar el tar.gz de logs ----------
$candidates = @(`
    Get-ChildItem -Path $Downloads -Filter "*logs*1031*.tar.gz" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
    Get-ChildItem -Path $Downloads -Filter "*logs*.tar.gz" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
    Get-ChildItem -Path $Downloads -Filter "*1031*.tar.gz" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
    Get-ChildItem -Path $Downloads -Filter "*.tar.gz" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
)

    # Extraer con tar (Windows 10+)`

        Write-Host "Extracción completada." -ForegroundColor Green`

        Write-Host "Error al extraer con tar. Intentando método alternativo..." -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        # Fallback si tar falla`
        Copy-Item $tarFile.FullName -Destination (Join-Path $ExportDir $tarFile.Name)`

}

    ForEach-Object {`
        if ($_.Line -match 'balance=([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\s*USDC') {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                linea   = $_.LineNumber`
                balance = [decimal]::Parse(`
                    $Matches[1],`
                    [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture`
                )`
            }`
        }`
    }
$balanceChanges = @()
$previous = $null
foreach ($r in $balanceRows) {`
    if ($null -eq $previous -or $r.balance -ne $previous) {`
        if ($null -ne $previous) {`
            $balanceChanges += [PSCustomObject]@{`
                linea   = $r.linea`
                balance = $r.balance`
                cambio  = [math]::Round(`
                    [double]($r.balance - $previous), 6`
                )`
            }`
        }`
        $previous = $r.balance`
    }`
}
# Seleccionar las líneas exactas donde cambió el balance
$targetLines = [System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[int]]::new()
foreach ($r in $balanceChanges) {`
    [void]$targetLines.Add([int]$r.linea)`
}
# Recuperar 12 líneas antes y 12 después de cada cambio
Select-String -Path $log -Pattern '💰 balance=' -Context 12,12 |`
    Where-Object {`
        $targetLines.Contains([int]$_.LineNumber)`
    } |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        "========== CAMBIO DE BALANCE EN LINEA $($_.LineNumber) =========="`
        $_.Context.PreContext`
        $_.Line`
        $_.Context.PostContext`
        ""`
    } |`
    Set-Content -Path $out -Encoding UTF8
Write-Host "`n--- RESULTADO ---" -ForegroundColor Cyan
"Movimientos encontrados: $($balanceChanges.Count)"
"Contextos guardados: $out"
# ---------- 3. Buscar logs sueltos comunes del bot ----------
$SearchPaths = @(`
    $Downloads,`
    $Desktop,`
    (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "Documents"),`
    (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "Desktop"),`
    $PSScriptRoot`

    "*tw_log*.jsonl",`

    "*trades_log*.jsonl",`
    "*cycle_log*",`
    "*dataflow*",`
    "*side_log*",`
    "*flow*",`
    "*cb_log*",`
    "*post_loss*"`
)
$FoundLogs = @()
foreach ($path in $SearchPaths) {`
    if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { continue }`
    foreach ($pat in $InterestingPatterns) {`
        $FoundLogs += Get-ChildItem -Path $path -Filter $pat -Recurse -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |`
                      Where-Object { $_.Length -lt 200MB }   # evitar archivos enormes`
    }`
}
$FoundLogs = $FoundLogs | Sort-Object FullName -Unique
Write-Host "Logs adicionales encontrados: $($FoundLogs.Count)" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$LogsDir = Join-Path $ExportDir "bot_logs"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $LogsDir -Force | Out-Null
foreach ($f in $FoundLogs) {`
    $destName = $f.Name`
    # Evitar sobrescribir si hay nombres iguales`
    $i = 1`
    while (Test-Path (Join-Path $LogsDir $destName)) {`
        $destName = "$($f.BaseName)_$i$($f.Extension)"`
        $i++`
    }`
    Copy-Item $f.FullName -Destination (Join-Path $LogsDir $destName) -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
}
# ---------- 4. Resumen rápido de tamaño ----------
Write-Host "`n=== Resumen de lo recopilado ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-ChildItem -Path $ExportDir -Recurse -File | `
    Select-Object FullName, @{N='SizeMB';E={[math]::Round($_.Length/1MB,2)}} |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize

Write-Host "Archivo generado: $OutZip" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Tamaño: $sizeMB MB" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "`nSúbelo a GitHub o compártelo. Contiene los logs de FLOW/CB/TW + capturador." -ForegroundColor Yellow
# Limpiar temporal (opcional)

        "[LINEA $($_.LineNumber)] $($_.Line)"`

            ForEach-Object {`
                $dest = Join-Path $LogsDir $_.Name`

    $name = $_.Name`
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("--- Archivo: $name ($([math]::Round($_.Length/1KB,0)) KB) ---")`

    $content = Get-Content $_.FullName -Tail 800 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`

        $_ -match "FLOW|CB|TW|WIN|LOSS|LOSS|sigma|z=|cvd|pm_net|ret_rel|SKIP|BUY|RESULT"`
    }`
    if ($interesting) {`
        $interesting | Select-Object -Last 150 | ForEach-Object { [void]$sb.AppendLine($_) }`
    }`
    [void]$sb.AppendLine("")`
}
$sb.ToString() | Out-File $Summary -Encoding UTF8

    Select-Object @{N='Archivo';E={$_.FullName.Replace($ExportDir,"")}}, `
                  @{N='MB';E={[math]::Round($_.Length/1MB,2)}} |`
    Format-Table -AutoSize

$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"

cat > analizar_volatilidad_3_metodos.py <<'PY'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import gzip
import json
import csv
import glob
import os
import math
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
# Busca automáticamente el microscopio y los registros auxiliares.
ROOT = "."
PATTERNS = [
    "*microscopio*.jsonl",`
    "*microscopio*.jsonl.gz",`
    "*eventos_estrategias*.csv",`
    "*operaciones_cb_flow_tw*.csv",`
]
FILES = []
for pattern in PATTERNS:
    FILES.extend(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, pattern)))
FILES = sorted(set(FILES))
OUT = "analisis_volatilidad_3_metodos"
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
METHODS = ("CB", "FLOW", "TW")
VOL_FIELDS = [
    "sigma", "volatility", "vol", "range_so_far",`
    "range_so_far_pct", "range_usd", "range_pct"
]
FEATURES = [
    "sigma", "range_so_far", "range_so_far_pct",`
    "vel_15s", "vel_30s", "vel_60s", "accel",`
    "cvd_30s", "cvd_60s", "cvd_perp_30s",`
    "cvd_cb_30s", "book_imb_now", "book_imb_30s",`
    "spread_now", "cb_premium_now", "pm_net",`
    "cvd_bn60", "v60", "z", "dominance",`
    "ask_depth", "elapsed", "entry_ask"
]
def number(v):
    try:
        x = float(v)
        return x if math.isfinite(x) else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
def first(d, names):
    for name in names:
        if name in d and d[name] is not None:
            return d[name]
    return None
def load_file(path):
    opener = gzip.open if path.endswith(".gz") else open
    with opener(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                obj = json.loads(line)
                if isinstance(obj, dict):
                    yield obj
            except Exception:
                continue
def load_csv(path):
    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        yield from csv.DictReader(f)
def method_of(d):
    s = " ".join(str(d.get(k, "")) for k in
                 ("method", "strategy", "source", "kind", "signal", "rule")).upper()
    for m in METHODS:
        if m in s:
            return m
    return None
def result_of(d):
    s = " ".join(str(d.get(k, "")) for k in
                 ("result", "outcome", "status", "resolution", "kind", "event")).upper()
    if any(x in s for x in ("WON", "WIN", "GANAD", "PROFIT")):
        return "WIN"
    if any(x in s for x in ("LOST", "LOSS", "PERDID")):
        return "LOSS"
    if any(x in s for x in ("REJECT", "SKIP", "BLOCK", "NO_TRADE")):
        return "REJECT"
    if "SHADOW" in s or "PAPER" in s:
        return "PAPER"
    return None
def volatility(d):
    # Prioridad a sigma si está disponible.
    x = number(first(d, VOL_FIELDS))
    if x is None:
        return None
    return abs(x)
def vol_band(v):
    if v is None:
        return "SIN_SIGMA"
    if v < 4:
        return "NORMAL_<4"
    if v < 5:
        return "ALTA_4_5"
    if v <= 6:
        return "ALTA_5_6"
    return "MUY_ALTA_>6"
def slug_of(d):
    return str(first(d, ("slug", "market_slug", "market", "epoch")) or "")
def write_csv(name, rows):
    if not rows:
        return
    path = os.path.join(OUT, name)
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
all_rows = []
cycles = {}
files_seen = Counter()
for path in FILES:
    base = os.path.basename(path).lower()
    if "microscopio" in base and path.endswith((".gz", ".jsonl")):
        for d in load_file(path):
            files_seen[base] += 1
            slug = slug_of(d)
            kind = str(d.get("kind", "")).lower()
            if kind in ("cycle", "resolution") and slug:
                cycles.setdefault(slug, {}).update(d)
            m = method_of(d)
            result = result_of(d)
            # Los snapshots se guardan para contexto, no como operaciones.
            if m and (result or any(k in d for k in`
                   ("signal", "entry_ask", "side", "rule"))):
                row = dict(d)
                row["_method"] = m
                row["_result"] = result or "UNCLASSIFIED"
                row["_vol"] = volatility(d)
                row["_band"] = vol_band(row["_vol"])
                all_rows.append(row)
    elif base.endswith(".csv"):
        for d in load_csv(path):
            files_seen[base] += 1
            m = method_of(d)
            result = result_of(d)
            if m:
                row = dict(d)
                row["_method"] = m
                row["_result"] = result or "UNCLASSIFIED"
                row["_vol"] = volatility(d)
                row["_band"] = vol_band(row["_vol"])
                all_rows.append(row)
# Deduplicar eventos por método, mercado, resultado y tiempo.
dedup = {}
for r in all_rows:
rows = list(dedup.values())
# Resumen por método y volatilidad.
summary = defaultdict(Counter)
for r in rows:
    c = summary[(r["_method"], r["_band"])]
    c[r["_result"]] += 1
summary_rows = []
for (m, band), c in sorted(summary.items()):
    total = sum(c.values())
    wins, losses = c["WIN"], c["LOSS"]
    summary_rows.append({`
        "method": m, "volatility": band, "events": total,`
        "wins": wins, "losses": losses,`
        "papers": c["PAPER"], "rejections": c["REJECT"],`
        "unclassified": c["UNCLASSIFIED"],`
        "win_rate_resolved_pct":`
            round(100*wins/(wins+losses), 2) if wins+losses else None
    })
# Estadísticas de variables para WIN / LOSS / REJECT por método y banda.
groups = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
for r in rows:
    for feature in FEATURES:
        x = number(r.get(feature))
        if x is not None:
            groups[(r["_method"], r["_band"], r["_result"])][feature].append(x)
feature_rows = []
for (m, band, result), fields in sorted(groups.items()):
    for feature, values in fields.items():
        feature_rows.append({`
            "method": m, "volatility": band, "result": result,`
            "feature": feature, "n": len(values),`
            "median": round(statistics.median(values), 6),`
            "mean": round(statistics.mean(values), 6),`
            "p25": round(sorted(values)[int((len(values)-1)*.25)], 6),`
            "p75": round(sorted(values)[int((len(values)-1)*.75)], 6)`
        })
# Exportar eventos para poder auditar ejemplos concretos.
event_rows = []
for r in rows:
    out = {`
        "method": r["_method"], "result": r["_result"],`
        "volatility": r["_band"], "sigma": r["_vol"],`
        "slug": slug_of(r),`
        "side": first(r, ("side", "direction")),`
        "elapsed": first(r, ("elapsed", "elapsed_s")),`
        "entry_ask": first(r, ("entry_ask", "ask", "price")),`
        "pnl": first(r, ("pnl", "profit", "net_pnl")),`
        "reason": first(r, ("reason", "skip_reason", "reject_reason", "rule"))`
    }
    for f in FEATURES:
        out[f] = number(r.get(f))
    event_rows.append(out)
write_csv("01_resumen_metodo_volatilidad.csv", summary_rows)
write_csv("02_variables_win_loss_reject.csv", feature_rows)
write_csv("03_eventos_deduplicados.csv", event_rows)
# Informe de cobertura y limitaciones.
report = {`
    "files_found": FILES,`
    "records_by_file": dict(files_seen),`
    "events_after_dedup": len(rows),`
    "methods": sorted({r["_method"] for r in rows}),`
    "volatility_bands": sorted({r["_band"] for r in rows}),`
    "cycles_with_close_or_resolution": len(cycles),`
    "warning": (`
        "Este análisis no convierte snapshots en operaciones. "`
        "Los eventos sin resultado quedan UNCLASSIFIED. "`
        "Revisar que sigma sea la misma métrica en los tres métodos. "`
        "Las tasas solo usan WIN y LOSS identificados."`
    )`
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import gzip
import json
import csv
import glob
import os
import math
import statistics
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
# Busca automáticamente el microscopio y los registros auxiliares.
ROOT = "."
PATTERNS = [
    "*microscopio*.jsonl",`
    "*microscopio*.jsonl.gz",`
    "*eventos_estrategias*.csv",`
    "*operaciones_cb_flow_tw*.csv",`
]
FILES = []
for pattern in PATTERNS:
    FILES.extend(glob.glob(os.path.join(ROOT, pattern)))
FILES = sorted(set(FILES))
OUT = "analisis_volatilidad_3_metodos"
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
METHODS = ("CB", "FLOW", "TW")
VOL_FIELDS = [
    "sigma", "volatility", "vol", "range_so_far",`
    "range_so_far_pct", "range_usd", "range_pct"
]
FEATURES = [
    "sigma", "range_so_far", "range_so_far_pct",`
    "vel_15s", "vel_30s", "vel_60s", "accel",`
    "cvd_30s", "cvd_60s", "cvd_perp_30s",`
    "cvd_cb_30s", "book_imb_now", "book_imb_30s",`
    "spread_now", "cb_premium_now", "pm_net",`
    "cvd_bn60", "v60", "z", "dominance",`
    "ask_depth", "elapsed", "entry_ask"
]
def number(v):
    try:
        x = float(v)
        return x if math.isfinite(x) else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
def first(d, names):
    for name in names:
        if name in d and d[name] is not None:
            return d[name]
    return None
def load_file(path):
    opener = gzip.open if path.endswith(".gz") else open
    with opener(path, "rt", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                obj = json.loads(line)
                if isinstance(obj, dict):
                    yield obj
            except Exception:
                continue
def load_csv(path):
    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        yield from csv.DictReader(f)
def method_of(d):
    s = " ".join(str(d.get(k, "")) for k in
                 ("method", "strategy", "source", "kind", "signal", "rule")).upper()
    for m in METHODS:
        if m in s:
            return m
    return None
def result_of(d):
    s = " ".join(str(d.get(k, "")) for k in
                 ("result", "outcome", "status", "resolution", "kind", "event")).upper()
    if any(x in s for x in ("WON", "WIN", "GANAD", "PROFIT")):
        return "WIN"
    if any(x in s for x in ("LOST", "LOSS", "PERDID")):
        return "LOSS"
    if any(x in s for x in ("REJECT", "SKIP", "BLOCK", "NO_TRADE")):
        return "REJECT"
    if "SHADOW" in s or "PAPER" in s:
        return "PAPER"
    return None
def volatility(d):
    # Prioridad a sigma si está disponible.
    x = number(first(d, VOL_FIELDS))
    if x is None:
        return None
    return abs(x)
def vol_band(v):
    if v is None:
        return "SIN_SIGMA"
    if v < 4:
        return "NORMAL_<4"
    if v < 5:
        return "ALTA_4_5"
    if v <= 6:
        return "ALTA_5_6"
    return "MUY_ALTA_>6"
def slug_of(d):
    return str(first(d, ("slug", "market_slug", "market", "epoch")) or "")
def write_csv(name, rows):
    if not rows:
        return
    path = os.path.join(OUT, name)
    with open(path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)
all_rows = []
cycles = {}
files_seen = Counter()
for path in FILES:
    base = os.path.basename(path).lower()
    if "microscopio" in base and path.endswith((".gz", ".jsonl")):
        for d in load_file(path):
            files_seen[base] += 1
            slug = slug_of(d)
            kind = str(d.get("kind", "")).lower()
            if kind in ("cycle", "resolution") and slug:
                cycles.setdefault(slug, {}).update(d)
            m = method_of(d)
            result = result_of(d)
            # Los snapshots se guardan para contexto, no como operaciones.
            if m and (result or any(k in d for k in`
                   ("signal", "entry_ask", "side", "rule"))):
                row = dict(d)
                row["_method"] = m
                row["_result"] = result or "UNCLASSIFIED"
                row["_vol"] = volatility(d)
                row["_band"] = vol_band(row["_vol"])
                all_rows.append(row)
    elif base.endswith(".csv"):
        for d in load_csv(path):
            files_seen[base] += 1
            m = method_of(d)
            result = result_of(d)
            if m:
                row = dict(d)
                row["_method"] = m
                row["_result"] = result or "UNCLASSIFIED"
                row["_vol"] = volatility(d)
                row["_band"] = vol_band(row["_vol"])
                all_rows.append(row)
# Deduplicar eventos por método, mercado, resultado y tiempo.
dedup = {}
for r in all_rows:
rows = list(dedup.values())
# Resumen por método y volatilidad.
summary = defaultdict(Counter)
for r in rows:
    c = summary[(r["_method"], r["_band"])]
    c[r["_result"]] += 1
summary_rows = []
for (m, band), c in sorted(summary.items()):
    total = sum(c.values())
    wins, losses = c["WIN"], c["LOSS"]
    summary_rows.append({`
        "method": m, "volatility": band, "events": total,`
        "wins": wins, "losses": losses,`
        "papers": c["PAPER"], "rejections": c["REJECT"],`
        "unclassified": c["UNCLASSIFIED"],`
        "win_rate_resolved_pct":`
            round(100*wins/(wins+losses), 2) if wins+losses else None
    })
# Estadísticas de variables para WIN / LOSS / REJECT por método y banda.
groups = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
for r in rows:
    for feature in FEATURES:
        x = number(r.get(feature))
        if x is not None:
            groups[(r["_method"], r["_band"], r["_result"])][feature].append(x)
feature_rows = []
for (m, band, result), fields in sorted(groups.items()):
    for feature, values in fields.items():
        feature_rows.append({`
            "method": m, "volatility": band, "result": result,`
            "feature": feature, "n": len(values),`
            "median": round(statistics.median(values), 6),`
            "mean": round(statistics.mean(values), 6),`
            "p25": round(sorted(values)[int((len(values)-1)*.25)], 6),`
            "p75": round(sorted(values)[int((len(values)-1)*.75)], 6)`
        })
# Exportar eventos para poder auditar ejemplos concretos.
event_rows = []
for r in rows:
    out = {`
        "method": r["_method"], "result": r["_result"],`
        "volatility": r["_band"], "sigma": r["_vol"],`
        "slug": slug_of(r),`
        "side": first(r, ("side", "direction")),`
        "elapsed": first(r, ("elapsed", "elapsed_s")),`
        "entry_ask": first(r, ("entry_ask", "ask", "price")),`
        "pnl": first(r, ("pnl", "profit", "net_pnl")),`
        "reason": first(r, ("reason", "skip_reason", "reject_reason", "rule"))`
    }
    for f in FEATURES:
        out[f] = number(r.get(f))
    event_rows.append(out)
write_csv("01_resumen_metodo_volatilidad.csv", summary_rows)
write_csv("02_variables_win_loss_reject.csv", feature_rows)
write_csv("03_eventos_deduplicados.csv", event_rows)
# Informe de cobertura y limitaciones.
report = {`
    "files_found": FILES,`
    "records_by_file": dict(files_seen),`
    "events_after_dedup": len(rows),`
    "methods": sorted({r["_method"] for r in rows}),`
    "volatility_bands": sorted({r["_band"] for r in rows}),`
    "cycles_with_close_or_resolution": len(cycles),`
    "warning": (`
        "Este análisis no convierte snapshots en operaciones. "`
        "Los eventos sin resultado quedan UNCLASSIFIED. "`
        "Revisar que sigma sea la misma métrica en los tres métodos. "`
        "Las tasas solo usan WIN y LOSS identificados."`
    )`
}
with open(os.path.join(OUT, "00_cobertura.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(report, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
print("\n=== ANALISIS CB + FLOW + TW ===")
print("Archivos detectados:", len(FILES))
print("Eventos únicos:", len(rows))
print("Métodos:", report["methods"])
print("\nResumen:")
for r in summary_rows:
    print(r)
print("\nResultados guardados en:", os.path.abspath(OUT))
print("Comparte estos archivos:")
print("  00_cobertura.json")
print("  01_resumen_metodo_volatilidad.csv")
print("  02_variables_win_loss_reject.csv")
print("  03_eventos_deduplicados.csv")

    members = [m for m in tar.getmembers() if m.isfile()]`

`
        for raw in f:`
            total += 1`
            line = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()`
`
            if not line:`
                continue`
`
            try:`
                obj = json.loads(line)`
                kind = str(obj.get("kind", obj.get("event", obj.get("type", "SIN_TIPO"))))`
                counts[kind] += 1`
            except Exception:`
                errors += 1`
                obj = None`
`
            if any(t in line for t in targets):`
                if len(matched) < 30:`
                    matched.append(line)`
`
        print("\n" + "=" * 75)`
        print("ARCHIVO:", m.name)`
        print("LINEAS:", total, "| ERRORES JSON:", errors)`
        print("TIPOS DE EVENTO:")`
        for k, v in counts.most_common(20):`
            print(f"  {k}: {v}")`
`
        print("\nREGISTROS DE LOS DOS CICLOS:")`
        for line in matched:`
            print(line[:5000])`
`

$targets = @(`
    @{`
        File = "microscopio_20261101_0500.jsonl.gz"`
        Entry = 1793534889.6`
        Method = "CB"`
    },`
    @{`
        File = "microscopio_20261101_0700.jsonl.gz"`
        Entry = 1793542625.2`
        Method = "FLOW"`
    }`
)
foreach ($x in $targets) {`
    Write-Host "`n========== $($x.Method) ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    $path = Join-Path $Root $x.File`
    $fs = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($path)`
    $gz = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $fs,`
        [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($gz)`
`
    try {`
        while ($null -ne ($line = $reader.ReadLine())) {`
            try {`
                $o = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
`
                if (`
                    $o.t -ge ($x.Entry - 12) -and`
                    $o.t -le ($x.Entry + 12) -and`
                    (`
                        ($o.s -eq "rtds" -and $o.e -eq "ds") -or`
                        ($o.s -eq "cl" -and`
                         ($o.e -eq "lectura" -or $o.e -eq "round"))`
                    )`
                ) {`
                    [PSCustomObject]@{`
                        Metodo = $x.Method`
                        Segundos = [math]::Round(`
                            [double]$o.t - $x.Entry, 2`
                        )`
                        Captura = $o.t`
                        Fuente = "$($o.s)/$($o.e)"`
                        Datos = ($o.d | ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 8)`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
            catch {}`
        }`
    }`
    finally {`
        $reader.Dispose()`
        $gz.Dispose()`
        $fs.Dispose()`
    }`
}

Write-Host "`n========== RESULT.JSON ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan
if (!(Test-Path $File)) {`
    Write-Host "No se encontró result.json" -ForegroundColor Red`
    return`
}
Get-Item $File | Select-Object Name, Length, LastWriteTime
$reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($File)
try {`
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt 30; $i++) {`
        $line = $reader.ReadLine()`
        if ($null -eq $line) { break }`
`
        if ($line.Length -gt 3000) {`
            $line = $line.Substring(0, 3000) + " ... [TRUNCADO]"`
        }`
`
        Write-Host "Línea $($i + 1): $line"`
    }`
}`
finally {`
    $reader.Dispose()`
}
$Root = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta"
$File = Join-Path $Root "result.json"
# Cargar la exportación de Telegram
Write-Host "`nCargando result.json..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
$Data = Get-Content -LiteralPath $File -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$Targets = @(`
    "1793534700",`
    "1793542500"`
)
foreach ($Target in $Targets) {`
    Write-Host "`n========== CICLO $Target ==========" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
`
    $Found = 0`
`
    foreach ($Message in $Data.messages) {`
        $Text = ""`
`
        if ($Message.text -is [array]) {`
            foreach ($Part in $Message.text) {`
                if ($Part -is [string]) {`
                    $Text += $Part`
                }`
                elseif ($Part.text) {`
                    $Text += $Part.text`
                }`
            }`
        }`
        elseif ($Message.text -is [string]) {`
            $Text = $Message.text`
        }`
`
        if ($Text.Contains($Target)) {`
            $Found++`
`
            Write-Host "`n--------------------------------"`
            Write-Host "Fecha: $($Message.date)"`
            Write-Host "Unix:  $($Message.date_unixtime)"`
            Write-Host "De:    $($Message.from)"`
            Write-Host "Texto:"`
            Write-Host $Text`
        }`
    }`
`
    if ($Found -eq 0) {`
        Write-Host "No se encontraron mensajes con ese identificador."`
    }`
    else {`
        Write-Host "`nMensajes encontrados: $Found" -ForegroundColor Green`
    }`
}
$Root = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta\Nueva carpeta (2)"
$Targets = @(`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Metodo = "CB"`
        Epoch = 1793534889.6`
        Archivo = "microscopio_20261101_0500.jsonl.gz"`
    },`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Metodo = "FLOW"`
        Epoch = 1793542625.2`
        Archivo = "microscopio_20261101_0700.jsonl.gz"`
    }`
)
foreach ($Target in $Targets) {`
    $Path = Join-Path $Root $Target.Archivo`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $($Target.Metodo) ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    Write-Host "Entrada Unix: $($Target.Epoch)"`
    Write-Host "Archivo: $($Target.Archivo)"`
`
    if (!(Test-Path $Path)) {`
        Write-Host "NO SE ENCONTRO EL ARCHIVO: $Path" -ForegroundColor Red`
        continue`
    }`
`
    $Stream = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($Path)`
    $Gzip = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $Stream,`
        [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $Reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($Gzip)`
`
    $Events = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()`
`
    try {`
        while ($null -ne ($Line = $Reader.ReadLine())) {`
            try {`
                $Obj = $Line | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop`
`
                if ($null -eq $Obj.t -or $null -eq $Obj.s -or $null -eq $Obj.e) {`
                    continue`
                }`
`
                $Diff = [double]$Obj.t - [double]$Target.Epoch`
`
                if ([Math]::Abs($Diff) -le 15) {`
                    $SourceEvent = "$($Obj.s)/$($Obj.e)"`
`
                    if (`
                        $SourceEvent -in @(`
                            "rtds/ds",`
                            "cl/lectura",`
                            "cl/round"`
                        )`
                    ) {`
                        $Utc = [DateTimeOffset]::FromUnixTimeMilliseconds(`
                            [long]([double]$Obj.t * 1000)`
                        ).UtcDateTime.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.fff")`
`
                        $Events.Add([PSCustomObject]@{`
                            SegundosRespectoEntrada = [Math]::Round($Diff, 3)`
                            TimestampUnix = $Obj.t`
                            UTC = $Utc`
                            Fuente = $SourceEvent`
                            Datos = ConvertTo-Json -InputObject $Obj.d -Depth 20 -Compress`
                        })`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
            catch {`
                continue`
            }`
        }`
    }`
    finally {`
        $Reader.Dispose()`
        $Gzip.Dispose()`
        $Stream.Dispose()`
    }`
`
    if ($Events.Count -eq 0) {`
        Write-Host "No se encontraron eventos de esas fuentes en +/-15 segundos." -ForegroundColor Yellow`
    }`
    else {`
        $Events |`
            Sort-Object TimestampUnix |`
            Format-Table SegundosRespectoEntrada, UTC, Fuente, Datos -Wrap |`
            Out-String -Width 300 |`
            Write-Host`
`
        Write-Host "Eventos encontrados: $($Events.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green`
    }`
}

        Write-Host "No localizado dentro de la carpeta indicada." -ForegroundColor Red`
    }`
}

    },`

    }`
)

    $Path = Join-Path $Root $Target.Archivo`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $($Target.Metodo) ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
    Write-Host "Entrada Unix: $($Target.Epoch)"`
`
    $Stream = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($Path)`
    $Gzip = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $Stream,`
        [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $Reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($Gzip)`
`
    $Events = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()`
`
    try {`
        while ($null -ne ($Line = $Reader.ReadLine())) {`
            try {`
                $Obj = $Line | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop`
`
                if ($null -eq $Obj.t -or $null -eq $Obj.s -or $null -eq $Obj.e) {`
                    continue`
                }`
`
                $Diff = [double]$Obj.t - [double]$Target.Epoch`
`
                if ([Math]::Abs($Diff) -le 15) {`
                    $SourceEvent = "$($Obj.s)/$($Obj.e)"`
`
                    if ($SourceEvent -in @("rtds/ds", "cl/lectura", "cl/round")) {`
                        $Events.Add([PSCustomObject]@{`
                            Segundos = [Math]::Round($Diff, 3)`
                            Timestamp = $Obj.t`
                            Fuente = $SourceEvent`
                            Datos = ConvertTo-Json -InputObject $Obj.d -Depth 20 -Compress`
                        })`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
            catch {`
                continue`
            }`
        }`
    }`
    finally {`
        $Reader.Dispose()`
        $Gzip.Dispose()`
        $Stream.Dispose()`
    }`
`
    if ($Events.Count -gt 0) {`
        $Events |`
            Sort-Object Timestamp |`
            Format-Table Segundos, Fuente, Datos -Wrap |`
            Out-String -Width 300 |`
            Write-Host`
`
        Write-Host "Total de eventos: $($Events.Count)" -ForegroundColor Green`
    }`
    else {`
        Write-Host "Sin eventos encontrados en esa ventana." -ForegroundColor Yellow`
    }`
}
$Root = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta"
$Targets = @(`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Metodo = "CB"`
        Epoch = 1793534700`
        PTB_Watcher = 77883.52`
        Archivo = "microscopio_20261101_0500.jsonl.gz"`
    },`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Metodo = "FLOW"`
        Epoch = 1793542500`
        PTB_Watcher = 77944.59`
        Archivo = "microscopio_20261101_0700.jsonl.gz"`
    }`
)
foreach ($Target in $Targets) {`
    $Path = Join-Path $Root $Target.Archivo`
    $Stream = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($Path)`
    $Gzip = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $Stream,`
        [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $Reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($Gzip)`
`
    $Prices = [System.Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()`
`
    try {`
        while ($null -ne ($Line = $Reader.ReadLine())) {`
            try {`
                $Obj = $Line | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop`
`
                if ($Obj.s -eq "rtds" -and $Obj.e -eq "ds") {`
                    $T = [double]$Obj.t`
`
                    if ($T -ge ($Target.Epoch - 60) -and $T -lt $Target.Epoch) {`
                        $V = [double]$Obj.d.v`
                        $Prices.Add([PSCustomObject]@{`
                            T = $T`
                            V = $V`
                        })`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
            catch {`
                continue`
            }`
        }`
    }`
    finally {`
        $Reader.Dispose()`
        $Gzip.Dispose()`
        $Stream.Dispose()`
    }`
`
    Write-Host "`n========== $($Target.Metodo) ==========" -ForegroundColor Cyan`
`
    if ($Prices.Count -eq 0) {`
        Write-Host "No hay muestras DS en los 60 segundos previos." -ForegroundColor Red`
        continue`
    }`
`
    $Values = @($Prices | ForEach-Object { $_.V })`
    $Average = ($Values | Measure-Object -Average).Average`
    $Min = ($Values | Measure-Object -Minimum).Minimum`
    $Max = ($Values | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum`
    $First = $Prices | Sort-Object T | Select-Object -First 1`
    $Last = $Prices | Sort-Object T | Select-Object -Last 1`
`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Metodo = $Target.Metodo`
        Muestras_DS = $Prices.Count`
        PTB_Watcher = $Target.PTB_Watcher`
        PTB_Promedio_DS = [Math]::Round($Average, 4)`
        Diferencia_Promedio_vs_Watcher = [Math]::Round($Average - $Target.PTB_Watcher, 4)`
        Min_DS = [Math]::Round($Min, 4)`
        Max_DS = [Math]::Round($Max, 4)`
        Primera_Muestra = [Math]::Round($First.T, 3)`
        Ultima_Muestra = [Math]::Round($Last.T, 3)`
        Segundos_Desde_Ultima_hasta_inicio = [Math]::Round($Target.Epoch - $Last.T, 3)`
    } | Format-List`
}

$base = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (3)\Nueva carpeta"
$markets = @("1793534700", "1793542500")   # CB y FLOW
Write-Host "=== ARCHIVOS ENCONTRADOS ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-ChildItem -Path $base -Recurse -File | Select-Object FullName, Length, LastWriteTime | Format-Table -AutoSize
Write-Host "`n=== RESULT.JSON (ciclos de interés) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$resultFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path $base -Recurse -Filter "*result*.json" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
foreach ($f in $resultFiles) {`
    Write-Host "`n--- $($f.FullName) ---"`
    try {`
        $data = Get-Content $f.FullName -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json`
        # Si es array o tiene ciclos`
        $cycles = if ($data -is [array]) { $data } else { $data.cycles ?? $data.results ?? $data }`
        foreach ($m in $markets) {`
            $match = $cycles | Where-Object { `
                ($_ | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5 -Compress) -match $m -or `
                $_.slug -match $m -or $_.market -match $m -or $_.id -match $m -or $_.start -match $m`
            }`
            if ($match) {`
                Write-Host ">>> MATCH mercado $m" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
                $match | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8`
            }`
        }`
    } catch { Write-Host "Error parseando $($f.Name): $_" }`
}
Write-Host "`n=== LOGS (líneas con los mercados + BUY/SELL/LOSS/CB/FLOW/PTB) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$logFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path $base -Recurse -Include *.log,*.txt,*.jsonl -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
foreach ($f in $logFiles) {`
    $hits = Select-String -Path $f.FullName -Pattern ($markets -join "|") -Context 3,8 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`
    if ($hits) {`
        Write-Host "`n--- $($f.FullName) ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        $hits | ForEach-Object { $_.Context.PreContext + $_.Line + $_.Context.PostContext }`
    }`
}
Write-Host "`n=== MICROSCOPIO / DATAFLOW (archivos de 30 min que contengan los mercados) ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan

foreach ($f in $microFiles) {`
    $content = Get-Content $f.FullName -Raw -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue`

        Write-Host "`n--- $($f.FullName) (primeras y últimas líneas + matches) ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow`

        Write-Host "..."`

    }`
}

`
        $texto = if ($m.text -is [array]) {`
            ($m.text | ForEach-Object {`
                if ($_ -is [string]) { $_ }`
                elseif ($_.text) { $_.text }`
            }) -join ""`

        }`

            $texto -match "PTB" -and`
            $texto -match "watcher|aplicado|exacto|exacta"`
        )`
`
        if ($esObjetivo -or $esConfirmacion) {`
            "FECHA: $($m.date)"`
            "UNIX:  $($m.date_unixtime)"`
            "TEXTO: $texto"`
            "----------------------------------------"`
        }`
    }`
}
