        $unix = 0.0`
        if (-not [double]::TryParse(`
            [string]$m.date_unixtime,`
            [ref]$unix`
        )) { continue }`
`
        if ($unix -lt ($o.Inicio - 120) -or`
            $unix -gt ($o.Inicio + 240)) { continue }`
`
        $texto = if ($m.text -is [array]) {`
            ($m.text | ForEach-Object {`
                if ($_ -is [string]) { $_ }`
                elseif ($_.text) { $_.text }`
            }) -join ""`
        } else {`
            [string]$m.text`
        }`
`
        if ($texto -match "PTB|watcher|aplicado|Nuevo ciclo|1793534700|1793542500|CB BUY|FLOW BUY|CB LOST|FLOW LOST") {`
            "HORA: $($m.date)"`
            "UNIX: $unix"`
            $texto`
            "--------------------------------"`
        }`
    }`
}
$salida | Out-File ".\auditoria_ptb_reducida.txt" -Encoding utf8
Get-Item ".\auditoria_ptb_reducida.txt" |`
    Select-Object Name, Length
$casos = @(`
    @{`
        Nombre = "CB"`
        Archivo = ".\microscopio_20261101_0500.jsonl.gz"`
        Entrada = 1793534889.68`
    },`
    @{`
        Nombre = "FLOW"`
        Archivo = ".\microscopio_20261101_0700.jsonl.gz"`
        Entrada = 1793542625.33`
    }`
)
foreach ($c in $casos) {`
`
    "`n========== $($c.Nombre) =========="`
`
    $fs = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead(`
        (Resolve-Path $c.Archivo)`
    )`
    $gz = [System.IO.Compression.GzipStream]::new(`
        $fs,`
        [System.IO.Compression.CompressionMode]::Decompress`
    )`
    $reader = [System.IO.StreamReader]::new($gz)`
`
    $muestras = @()`
`
    while ($null -ne ($linea = $reader.ReadLine())) {`
        if ($linea -notmatch '"rtds"') { continue }`
`
        $r = $linea | ConvertFrom-Json`
`
        if (`
            $r.e -eq "ds" -and`
            [math]::Abs($r.t - $c.Entrada) -le 5`
        ) {`
            $muestras += $r`
        }`
    }`
`
    $reader.Close()`
    $gz.Dispose()`
    $fs.Dispose()`
`
    $muestras |`
        Sort-Object t |`
        ForEach-Object {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                Captura_Unix = $_.t`
                Diferencia_Entrada = [math]::Round(`
                    $_.t - $c.Entrada, 3`
                )`
                Payload_Completo = ($_.d | ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 10)`
            }`
        } |`
        Format-List`
}

    }`

        # Aquí puedes hacer análisis detallado, por ejemplo:`
        # Mostrar el registro completo o filtrar según condiciones específicas`
        Write-Output $line`
    }`
}

$logDir = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (4)\min_1859"
$lateLogFile = Join-Path $logDir "late_log.jsonl"
$startEpoch = 1794215100
$foundStart = $false
Get-Content $lateLogFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ($line -and $line -ne "") {`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            # Extraer epoch desde slug si existe`
            $epoch = $null`
            if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
                $slug = $obj.slug`
                if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                    $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                }`
            }`
            if (-not $foundStart) {`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch) {`
                    $foundStart = $true`
                    Write-Output "======= Análisis desde mercado $epoch ======="`
                } else {`
                    return`
                }`
            }`
`
            # Mostrar eventos LATE o pérdidas (pnl negativo si existe)`
            $isLate = ($obj.kind -like '*LATE*') -or ($obj.method -like '*LATE*')`
            $isLoss = ($obj.pnl -lt 0)`
`
            if ($isLate -or $isLoss) {`
                [PSCustomObject]@{`
                    Timestamp = if ($obj.ts) { `
                        try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null }`
                    } else { $null }`
                    Slug      = $obj.slug`
                    Side      = $obj.side`
                    Price     = $obj.price`
                    PnL       = $obj.pnl`
                    Reason    = $obj.reason`
                    Kind      = $obj.kind`
                    Method    = $obj.method`
                    Detail    = $obj.detail`
                }`
            }`
        } catch {`
            # Ignorar línea inválida`
        }`
    }`
}
$logDir = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (4)\min_1859\min_1859"
$signalsFile = Join-Path $logDir "signals_log.jsonl"
$startEpoch = 1794215100
$strategies = @("QUIET", "TW", "LATE", "POLEX", "CB", "FLOW")
$foundStart = $false
Get-Content $signalsFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ($line -and $line -ne "") {`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            $epoch = $null`
            if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
                $slug = $obj.slug`
                if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                    $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                }`
            }`
            if (-not $foundStart) {`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch) {`
                    $foundStart = $true`
                    Write-Output "======= Análisis desde mercado $epoch ======="`
                } else {`
                    return`
                }`
            }`
`
            if ($obj.decision -eq "BUY") {`
                foreach ($strat in $strategies) {`
                    if ($obj.side -and $strat -and ($obj.reject_category -eq $strat.ToUpper() -or ($obj.method -like "*$strat*") -or ($obj.kind -like "*$strat*"))) {`
                        [PSCustomObject]@{`
                            Timestamp       = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                            Slug            = $obj.slug`
                            Side            = $obj.side`
                            Price           = $obj.entry_price`
                            Outcome         = if ($obj.won -ne $null) { if ($obj.won) { "Win" } else { "Loss" } } else { "Pending" }`
                            PnL             = $obj.pnl`
                            Strategy        = $strat`
                            Decision        = $obj.decision`
                            RejectCategory  = $obj.reject_category`
                            Reason          = $obj.exit_reason`
                        }`
                        break`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
        } catch {`
            # Ignorar líneas no válidas`
        }`
    }`
}
if ($obj.decision -eq "BUY") {`
    [PSCustomObject]@{`
        Timestamp       = ...`
        Slug            = $obj.slug`
        Side            = $obj.side`
        Price           = $obj.entry_price`
        Outcome         = if ($obj.won -ne $null) { if ($obj.won) { "Win" } else { "Loss" } } else { "Pending" }`
        PnL             = $obj.pnl`
        Decision        = $obj.decision`
        RejectCategory  = $obj.reject_category`
        Method          = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "method") { $obj.method } else { "" }`
        Kind            = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "kind") { $obj.kind } else { "" }`
    }`
}

$logDir = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (4)\min_1859\min_1859"
$signalsFile = Join-Path $logDir "signals_log.jsonl"
$startEpoch = 1794215100
$strategies = @("QUIET", "TW", "LATE", "POLEX", "CB", "FLOW")
$foundStart = $false
Get-Content $signalsFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ($line -and $line -ne "") {`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            $epoch = $null`
            if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
                $slug = $obj.slug`
                if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                    $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                }`
            }`
            if (-not $foundStart) {`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch) {`
                    $foundStart = $true`
                    Write-Output "======= Análisis desde mercado $epoch ======="`
                } else {`
                    return`
                }`
            }`
`
            # Filtrar por estrategias en reject_category o decision que contengan nombre en mayúsculas`
            $foundStrat = $false`
            foreach ($strat in $strategies) {`
                if (($obj.reject_category -and $obj.reject_category.ToUpper().Contains($strat)) -or`
                    ($obj.decision -and $obj.decision.ToUpper().Contains($strat)) -or`
                    ($obj.kind -and $obj.kind.ToUpper().Contains($strat)) -or`
                    ($obj.method -and $obj.method.ToUpper().Contains($strat))) {`
                    $foundStrat = $true`
                    break`
                }`
            }`
`
            if ($foundStrat) {`
                # Mostrar información clave (tanto compras como rechazos)`
                [PSCustomObject]@{`
                    Timestamp       = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                    Slug            = $obj.slug`
                    Side            = $obj.side`
                    Price           = $obj.entry_price`
                    Outcome         = if ($obj.won -ne $null) { if ($obj.won) { "Win" } else { "Loss" } } else { "Pending" }`
                    PnL             = $obj.pnl`
                    Decision        = $obj.decision`
                    RejectCategory  = $obj.reject_category`
                    Kind            = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "kind") { $obj.kind } else { "" }`
                    Method          = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "method") { $obj.method } else { "" }`
                }`
            }`
        } catch {`
            # Ignorar línea inválida`
        }`
    }`
}
$logDir = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (4)\min_1859\min_1859"
$startEpoch = 1794215100
$methods = @("quiet", "tw", "late", "polex", "cb", "flow")
foreach ($method in $methods) {`
    $logFile = Join-Path $logDir "$method`_log.jsonl"`
    if (-Not (Test-Path $logFile)) {`
        Write-Warning "Archivo no encontrado: $logFile"`
        continue`
    }`
    Write-Output "`n======= Análisis método $method ======="`
    $foundStart = $false`
`
    Get-Content $logFile | ForEach-Object {`
        $line = $_.Trim()`
        if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            $epoch = $null`
            if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
                $slug = $obj.slug`
                if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                    $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                }`
            }`
            if (-not $foundStart) {`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch) {`
                    $foundStart = $true`
                } else {`
                    return`
                }`
            }`
`
            # Filtrar intentos de compra => indep. campo 'decision', generalmente 'BUY' o señales positivas`
            # Algunos métodos pueden usar campos como 'side', 'price', 'pnl', etc.`
`
            # Consideramos registros que tengan 'decision' = 'BUY' o que estén relacionados (ej. intentos)`
            if ($obj.decision -and $obj.decision.ToUpper() -match "BUY") {`
                [PSCustomObject]@{`
                    Timestamp        = if ($obj.ts) { Try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } Catch { $null } } Else { $null }`
                    MarketSlug       = $obj.slug`
                    Side             = $obj.side`
                    EntryPrice       = $obj.entry_price`
                    Decision         = $obj.decision`
                    PnL              = $obj.pnl`
                    Outcome          = if ($obj.won -ne $null) { if ($obj.won) { "Win" } else { "Loss" } } else { "Pending" }`
                }`
            }`
        } catch {`
            # Ignorar lineas inválidas`
        }`
    }`
}
$logDir = "C:\Users\Paul campos\Desktop\Nueva carpeta (4)\min_1859\min_1859"
$startEpoch = 1794215100
$methods = @("quiet", "tw", "late", "polex", "cb", "flow")
function Get-EpochFromSlug([string]$slug) {`
    if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
        return [int]$matches[1]`
    }`
    return $null`
}
foreach ($method in $methods) {`
    $logFile = Join-Path $logDir "$method`_log.jsonl"`
    if (-not (Test-Path $logFile)) {`
        Write-Warning "Archivo no encontrado: $logFile"`
        continue`
    }`
`
    Write-Output "`n======= Muestreo método $method ======="`
    $foundStart = $false`
    $counter = 0`
`
    Get-Content $logFile | ForEach-Object {`
        if ($counter -ge 20) { return }  # Limitar muestreo a 20 registros por archivo`
        $line = $_.Trim()`
        if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            $epoch = $null`
            if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
                $slug = $obj.slug`
                if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                    $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                }`
            }`
            if (-not $foundStart) {`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch) {`
                    $foundStart = $true`
                }`
                else { return }`
            }`
            # Mostrar campos para inspección`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                Timestamp     = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                Slug          = $obj.slug`
                Side          = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "side") { $obj.side } else { $null }`
                Price         = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "price") { $obj.price } elseif ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "entry_price") { $obj.entry_price } else { $null }`
                Decision      = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "decision") { $obj.decision } else { $null }`
                PnL           = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "pnl") { $obj.pnl } else { $null }`
                Won           = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "won") { $obj.won } else { $null }`
                Type          = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "kind") { $obj.kind } elseif ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "method") { $obj.method } else { $null }`
                Raw           = $line`
            }`
            $counter++`
        } catch {`
            # Ignorar líneas inválidas`
        }`
    }`
}

# Primero leer órdenes reales compradas en trades_log.jsonl
$tradeLogFile = Join-Path $logDir "trades_log.jsonl"
Write-Output "======= Órdenes compradas reales a partir de mercado $startEpoch ======="
Get-Content $tradeLogFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
    try {`
        $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
        # extraer epoch del slug`
        $epoch = $null`
        if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
            $slug = $obj.slug`
            if ($slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
            }`
        }`
        if ($epoch -lt $startEpoch) { return }`
        if ($obj.action -eq "BUY") {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                Timestamp    = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                MarketSlug   = $obj.slug`
                Side         = $obj.side`
                Price        = $obj.price`
                Size         = $obj.size`
                USDC         = $obj.usdc`
                PnL          = if ($obj.pnl) { $obj.pnl } else { 0 }`
                Reason       = $obj.reason`
                Method       = "TRADE_LOG"`
            }`
        }`
    } catch {`
        # ignorar línea inválida`
    }`
}

Get-Content $tradesFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
    try {`
        $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
        $epoch = $null`
        if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug') {`
            if ($obj.slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
            }`
        }`
        if ($epoch -lt $startEpoch) { return }`
        if ($obj.action -eq "BUY" -and $obj.reason -match "LATE") {`
            [PSCustomObject]@{`
                Timestamp = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                MarketSlug = $obj.slug`
                Side = $obj.side`
                Price = $obj.price`
                Size = $obj.size`
                USDC = $obj.usdc`
                PnL = if ($obj.pnl) { $obj.pnl } else { 0 }`
                Outcome = if ($obj.pnl -gt 0) { "Win" } elseif ($obj.pnl -lt 0) { "Loss" } else { "Pending" }`
            }`
        }`
    }`
    catch {`
        # Ignorar lines invalidas`
    }`
}
$lateLogFile = Join-Path $logDir "late_log.jsonl"
# Carga en memoria las compras reales extraídas anteriormente (usa Bloque 1 para generar archivo o variable).
# Aquí asumo que crearás una lista llamada $lateBuys con Timestamp y Slug y Side de Bloque 1.
# Ejemplo de cómo cargar (ajusta si guardas en archivo):
# $lateBuys = Import-Csv "late_purchases.csv"
# Aquí solo un esquema para filtrar late_log por esos slugs y timestamp
$lateBuys = @() # Rellena con los datos del Bloque 1: lista de objetos con Timestamp, MarketSlug, Side
Get-Content $lateLogFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
    try {`
        $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
        foreach ($buy in $lateBuys) {`
            if ($obj.slug -eq $buy.MarketSlug) {`
                $logTs = if ($obj.ts) { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } else { $null }`
                $buyTs = $buy.Timestamp`
                # Comparar timestamps cercanos (ej, +/- 2 minutos)`
                if ($logTs -and $buyTs -and ([math]::Abs(($logTs - $buyTs).TotalSeconds) -le 120)) {`
                    # Mostrar info relevante para análisis técnico`
                    [PSCustomObject]@{`
                        Timestamp = $logTs`
                        MarketSlug = $obj.slug`
                        Side = $obj.side`
                        Type = $obj.kind`
                        Rule = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "rule") { $obj.rule } else { "" }`
                        Vel5 = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "vel5") { $obj.vel5 } else { $null }`
                        DS_Delta = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "ds_delta") { $obj.ds_delta } else { $null }`
                        Sigma = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "sigma") { $obj.sigma } else { $null }`
                        Z = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "z") { $obj.z } else { $null }`
                        V60 = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "v60") { $obj.v60 } else { $null }`
                        PM_Net_USD = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "pm_net_side_usd") { $obj.pm_net_side_usd } else { $null }`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
        }`
    }`
    catch {}`
}
$lateBuys = @()
Get-Content $tradesFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) { return }`
    try {`
        $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
        if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'slug' -and $obj.slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
            $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
            if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch -and $obj.action -eq "BUY" -and $obj.reason -match "LATE") {`
                $lateBuys += [PSCustomObject]@{`
                    Timestamp = if ($obj.ts) { try { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } catch { $null } } else { $null }`
                    MarketSlug = $obj.slug`
                    Side = $obj.side`
                    Price = $obj.price`
                    Size = $obj.size`
                    USDC = $obj.usdc`
                    PnL = if ($obj.pnl) { $obj.pnl } else { 0 }`
                }`
            }`
        }`
    } catch {}`
}

# La propiedad Reason contiene el método: LATE, TW, etc. Por ejemplo: "LATE_SIGNAL", "TF_TREND_UP", "FLOW", etc.
Get-Content $tradesFile | ForEach-Object {`
    $line = $_.Trim()`
    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty($line)) {`
        try {`
            $obj = $line | ConvertFrom-Json`
            if ($obj.slug -and $obj.slug -match '(\d+)$') {`
                $epoch = [int]$matches[1]`
                if ($epoch -ge $startEpoch -and $obj.action -eq "BUY") {`
                    # Filtrar solo compras LATE o las que contengan 'LATE' en Reason`
                    if ($obj.reason -and $obj.reason.ToUpper().Contains("LATE")) {`
                        [PSCustomObject]@{`
                            Timestamp       = if ($obj.ts) { [datetime]::FromFileTimeUtc([long]($obj.ts * 1e7)).ToLocalTime() } else { $null }`
                            MarketSlug      = $obj.slug`
                            Side            = $obj.side`
                            Price           = $obj.price`
                            Size            = $obj.size`
                            USDC            = $obj.usdc`
                            PnL             = if ($obj.pnl) { $obj.pnl } else { 0 }`
                            Reason          = $obj.reason`
                        }`
                    }`
                }`
            }`
        } catch {}`
    }`
}

                MarketSlug  = $obj.slug`
                Side        = $obj.side`
                Kind        = $obj.kind`
                Decision    = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "decision") { $obj.decision } else { "" }`
                OK          = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "ok") { $obj.ok } else { $null }`
                Try         = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "try") { $obj.try } else { $null }`
                Amount      = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "amount") { $obj.amount } else { $null }`
                FillPrice   = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "fill_price") { $obj.fill_price } else { $null }`
                Shares      = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "shares") { $obj.shares } else { $null }`
                Resp        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "resp") { $obj.resp } else { $null }`
                Rule        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "rule") { $obj.rule } else { "" }`
                Vel5        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "vel5") { $obj.vel5 } else { $null }`
                DS_Delta    = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "ds_delta") { $obj.ds_delta } else { $null }`
                Sigma       = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "sigma") { $obj.sigma } else { $null }`
                Z           = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "z") { $obj.z } else { $null }`
            }`
        } catch {}`
    }`
}

                MarketSlug  = $obj.slug`

                FillPrice   = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "fill_price") { $obj.fill_price } else { $null }`
                Shares      = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "shares") { $obj.shares } else { $null }`
                Resp        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "resp") { $obj.resp } else { $null }`
                Rule        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "rule") { $obj.rule } else { "" }`
                Vel5        = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "vel5") { $obj.vel5 } else { $null }`
                DS_Delta    = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "ds_delta") { $obj.ds_delta } else { $null }`
                Sigma       = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "sigma") { $obj.sigma } else { $null }`
                Z           = if ($obj.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains "z") { $obj.z } else { $null }`
            }`
            $record | Out-String | Add-Content $outputFile`
            Add-Content $outputFile "`n"  # línea en blanco para separar registros`
        } catch {}`
    }`
}

# Mostrar estructura (hasta 3 niveles de profundidad)
Write-Host "`n=== ESTRUCTURA DE LA CARPETA ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Get-ChildItem -Recurse -Depth 3 | `
    Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch 'node_modules|\.git|dist|build|\.next|out' } |`
    ForEach-Object {`
        $indent = "  " * ($_.FullName.Split('\').Count - (Get-Location).Path.Split('\').Count)`
        if ($_.PSIsContainer) {`
            Write-Host "$indent📁 $($_.Name)" -ForegroundColor Yellow`
        } else {`
            Write-Host "$indent📄 $($_.Name)" -ForegroundColor Gray`
        }`
    }
# Buscar archivos importantes
Write-Host "`n=== ARCHIVOS CLAVE ===" -ForegroundColor Cyan
$importantes = @("package.json", "netlify.toml", "vite.config.*", "next.config.*", "astro.config.*", "index.html", "README.md", ".gitignore")
Get-ChildItem -Recurse -File | Where-Object { `
    $name = $_.Name`
    $importantes | Where-Object { $name -like $_ }`
} | Select-Object FullName, Length, LastWriteTime | Format-Table -AutoSize

    Select-Object Name, FullName
